Our dataset contains annotations for a range of computer vision problems including 20,000 images with instance segmentation masks, 5000 images with multi-subject pose coordinates, over 800 video clips with action classes, and over 800 clips with detailed text description. This is the first such dataset to link this wide range of annotation types together for multi-modal use. We release this entire dataset, and our custom annotation tools will also be made available, in order to allow for ongoing expansion. We also provide a series of benchmarks for the tasks of Instance Segmentation, Pose detection, Temporal Action Localisation, and finally video-text retrieval. The totality of this multi-modal dataset is the data needed to solve both these individual computer vision tasks as well as enabling more complex multi-modal tasks making use of a combination of annotations.

</details>


### [70] [CoCode-World: Shared 3D Proxy Representation for Multi-Player World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15602)
*Qirui Li,Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Jinkun Hao,Paul L Rosin,Yu-Kun Lai,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Ran Yi,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoCode-World 提出了一种共享3D代理框架，将结构控制与视觉合成分开，用于两人视频生成。该模型利用预训练的视频模型的注意机制在单个生成分支内进行跨视角通信，同时通过 LoRA 调整学习代理的符合性和外观对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成具备一致性和多样性的多人生成性世界模型的挑战在于既要提供空间控制，又要确保从不同视角观察到的共享实体具有连贯性且不限制场景多样性。

Method: CoCode-World 利用3D代理分离结构控制与视觉合成，通过配对代理视频、外观参考和目标视图的一致空间布局，在预训练的视频模型中联合生成两个视角的视频，并通过 LoRA 调整学习视角之间的协调。

Result: 该方法在视频质量提升和从训练未见视角身份跨越多玩家身份方面表现出色，并通过两个玩家的 GTA V 数据集验证了其效果。还通过消融实验考察了3D代理构建和跨视角通信对生成的影响。

Conclusion: CoCode-World 为多人游戏视频生成提供了一种解决方案，它通过将结构控制与视觉合成分离，并利用3D代理和预训练模型的协同作用，实现自动生成高保真且多样化的多人视频。

Abstract: Multiplayer generative world models must produce observations that agree on shared entities despite different camera motions and occlusions. Explicit world representations provide spatial control, but precise modeling of individual game maps limits scene diversity, while shared geometry alone cannot ensure consistent appearance across views. We present CoCode-World, a shared 3D proxy framework that separates structural control from visual synthesis for two-player video generation. Programmable proxies specify scene layout, motion, and occlusion, while RGB references guide character and scene appearance. To coordinate visual details across players, we concatenate paired proxy videos, appearance references, and target views in a consistent spatial layout and generate both views jointly with a pretrained video model. This formulation uses the model's native attention for cross-view communication within a single generation branch, with LoRA adaptation learning proxy adherence and appearance correspondence from paired observations. We construct a two-player GTA~V dataset that pairs synchronized RGB observations with proxy videos derived from shared map geometry and game-engine state. Experiments demonstrate appearance transfer to characters and scene styles unseen during training, with improvements in video quality and cross-view player identity over the evaluated baseline. Ablations further examine how proxy construction and cross-view communication affect generation. Project page: https://iapple233.github.io/CCW/.

</details>


### [71] [Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning for Label-Efficient Agricultural Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15610)
*Hazem Ahmed,Ali Hamdi,Ammar Mohammed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种新的自监督学习方法，CBRL，它结合了对比学习和 BOOTSTRAP 方法，使用 ViT-L/16 作为主干，仅用10%的下游标签，在四个农业测试基准上实现了最高的宏观F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业图像分类依赖于专家标注，收集未标注的图像样本比获得可靠的标签更简单。自监督学习可以通过在未标注图像上预训练可迁移的表示来减轻这种依赖。

Method: CBRL 联合优化了 SimCLR 风格的非对称交叉熵对比学习和双向的 BYOL 风格的指数移动平均目标预测。使用ViT-L/16作为主干，并利用10%的下游标签进行训练。

Result: 在四个主要的农业测试基准上，CBRL 记录了最高的宏F1估计值。具体来说，在DeepWeeds数据集上，CBRL 达到了79.77%的测试宏观F1，比随机初始化基线高出32.29个百分点，比下一个最高的比较点高出15.78个百分点；在 PlantVillage 数据集上，CBRL 达到了98.14%的宏观F1，分别比基线和下一个最高点高出10.43和0.43个百分点；但 CBRL 在 PlantDoc 数据集上却无法达到实用性能。

Conclusion: 尽管 CBRL 在某些数据集上表现出色，但它仍需进一步研究以确定这种结合方法的效果是否稳定，以及是否具有统计优势。

Abstract: Expert annotation remains a major bottleneck in agricultural image classification, where collecting images is often easier than obtaining reliable labels from domain specialists. Self-supervised learning can reduce this dependence by pretraining transferable representations on unlabelled imagery. Contrastive and bootstrap methods provide two established but distinct training signals, motivating an empirical study of a formulation that applies both signals within one representation path under severe downstream label scarcity. We present Contrastive Bootstrap Representation Learning (CBRL), which jointly optimises SimCLR-style NT-Xent contrastive learning and bidirectional BYOL-style exponential-moving-average target prediction within a shared online projection space. Using a ViT-L/16 backbone and 10% of the downstream labels, we report single-seed point estimates on four primary agricultural test benchmarks plus an exploratory 33-image New Plant Diseases evaluation. CBRL records the highest macro-F1 point estimate on the four primary benchmarks. On DeepWeeds, its 79.77% test macro-F1 is 32.29 points above the random-initialisation, label-only baseline and 15.78 points above the next-highest comparison point estimate; on PlantVillage, its 98.14% macro-F1 is 10.43 and 0.43 points higher, respectively. On PlantDoc, however, all methods fail to reach practically useful performance. These results identify the evaluated joint formulation as a promising candidate for label-limited transfer, but they do not establish objective synergy, statistical superiority, or robustness across stochastic runs.

</details>


### [72] [Learning-Based Interpolation of Detector Gaps in Photon-Counting X-ray Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15614)
*Johannes B. Thalhammer,Patrick Ilg,Tina Dorosti,Sebastian Peterhansl,Theresa Hiu,Daniel Frey,Zijin Huang,Florian Schaff,Daniela Pfeiffer,Franz Pfeiffer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于处理图像检测器视场缺口的方法，通过适配两种生成模型（Wasserstein GAN和条件Pix2Pix）来填补这些缺口，同时保持其他像素的未修改和填充区域的强度统计状态。通过与线性插值、Navier-Stokes插值法和MADF深度图像填补网络的比较，证明了这两种模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像检测器中的物理缺口需要在后续处理（如相位检索或断层重建）之前被填充；然而，普通的图像填补方法不能应用于该场景，因为它们会导致环状伪影。

Method: 研究采用的两种生成模型是Wasserstein GAN和条件Pix2Pix，它们分别针对填充任务进行策略性的遮罩调整，并引入特定的损失函数来约束新填充区域的平均强度和强度分布。

Result: 两种模型在模拟数据上表现出优秀的感知相似性（LPIPS）和直方图距离，并且在生成时间上有优势。Pix2Pix的训练效率更高，需要更少的GPU小时。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，Pix2Pix模型在处理实际检测器缺口时最自然地填充了缺口，尽管与线性插值相比，重建精度仅略有提高。这项工作还提供了代码和最终模型的权重，方便其他研究者使用。

Abstract: Image detectors assembled from tiled modules have physical gaps in the field of view that must be filled before downstream processing such as phase retrieval or tomographic reconstruction. Unlike natural-image inpainting, detector-gap interpolation must leave all other pixels unmodified and preserve the intensity statistics of the filled region, since even a small shift in mean intensity can cause ring artifacts after reconstruction. We adapted two generative models, a Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty (WGAN-GP) and a conditional Pix2Pix model, to this task. We implemented a masking strategy that restricts all changes to the gap pixels and loss terms that constrain the mean intensity and the intensity distribution of the filled region. Both models were trained on nanoCT projections with simulated gaps and compared with linear interpolation, Navier--Stokes inpainting, and the deep image-inpainting network MADF, quantitatively on simulated gaps and visually on real detector gaps. WGAN-GP and Pix2Pix achieved the best perceptual similarity (LPIPS) and histogram distance and, apart from linear interpolation, the shortest inference time. Pix2Pix required about five times fewer training GPU-hours than WGAN-GP. Ablation studies showed that masking the network output contributes by far the most to plausible inpainting results. Applied to sinograms, Pix2Pix produced the most natural gap filling but improved the reconstruction only slightly compared with linear interpolation. The source code and the weights of the final Pix2Pix model are available at https://github.com/J-3TO/detectorGapRemoval_Pix2Pix.

</details>


### [73] [WaveletLoRA: Frequency-Aware Content-Style Decomposition for Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15617)
*Peiyao Wang,Jiahui Sun,Weining Wang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveletLoRA 提出了一种新的频率感知框架，通过小波域监督实现显式的内容-风格分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像内容-风格分解方法主要在空间域中操作，未能充分利用内容和风格固有的频域特性，旨在通过分离主体身份和视觉风格来实现灵活重组。

Method: WaveletLoRA 使用多层次离散小波变换分解参考图像为低频和高频子带，并为每个子带分配一个专门的 LoRA 分支，低频分支用于风格获取，高频分支用于内容保留。引入了密集的 Experts 混合模块和频域正则化目标以提高分离质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，WaveletLoRA 在内容-风格分解任务中表现超越现有方法，生成的结果更加符合人类的偏好。

Conclusion: WaveletLoRA 提供了一种有效的频率感知方法来实现内容-风格分解，增强了分离质量和内容保真度。

Abstract: Content-style decomposition from a single image is a fundamental challenge in personalized image generation, aiming to separate subject identity from visual style for flexible recomposition. Existing approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain, without explicitly exploiting the distinct frequency characteristics naturally associated with content and style. In this paper, we propose WaveletLoRA, a novel frequency-aware framework for explicit content-style decomposition through wavelet-domain supervision. Specifically, we first apply a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to decompose the reference image into low-frequency and high-frequency subbands. We then assign a dedicated LoRA branch to each subband, where the low-frequency branch models global appearance for style acquisition, while the high-frequency branches capture structural details for content preservation. To further improve disentanglement quality, we introduce a dense Mixture-of-Experts aggregation module together with a frequency-domain regularization objective, which reduces style leakage and enhance content fidelity. In addition, we establish WaveBench and develop a VLM-based evaluation protocol that explicitly measures both attribute similarity and attribute separation for disentanglement assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveletLoRA consistently outperforms existing methods and produces results that better align with human preference.

</details>


### [74] [MemInStream: Memorizing or Merely Recognizing? Rethinking Streaming Video Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15640)
*Zhenqi He,Dunyuan Xu,Yang Bo,Kien T. Pham,Yanghao Wang,Shuhan Li,Xintong Guo,Yuyang He,Fang Liu,Huankang Guan,Jingyan Chen,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种名为MemInStream的新基准，用于评估连续视频理解的记忆能力。通过证据控制验证协议，该基准能够区分理解和记忆能力的界限，涵盖了事件检索、状态跟踪与更新、多证据整合以及跨视频场景的上下文记忆四大类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频理解基准过于依赖问答准确性，无法可靠衡量记忆能力。MemInStream旨在解决这一问题，通过引入特定的验证协议，明确评估模型的记忆能力。

Method: MemInStream通过一种新的证据控制验证协议，将问题与其时间上的证据区间关联，并通过控制模型能否利用这些证据区间来解决问题，而不是依赖最近观察、证据后的观察或文本输入，从而评估模型的记忆需求。

Result: MemInStream通过四种主要类别（事件检索、状态跟踪与更新、多证据整合以及跨视频场景的上下文记忆）提供了一个全面和可靠的基准，以系统地评估连续视频理解中的记忆能力。

Conclusion: MemInStream代表了视频理解领域的一项重要进展，为研究者提供了一个更准确的工具来评估模型的记忆能力，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding aims to continuously process incoming visual observations, making the retention and retrieval of relevant information increasingly important as the video history grows. Although existing benchmarks include memory-related questions, question-answering accuracy alone does not reliably measure memory capabilities. Incorrect predictions may arise from failures in visual understanding rather than forgetting, whereas correct predictions may rely on linguistic priors or recent observations without requiring historical memory. To address these ambiguities, we introduce MemInStream, a benchmark dedicated to evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding. We develop an evidence-controlled validation protocol that associates each question with temporally localized evidence intervals. The protocol validates evidence solvability and memory necessity by retaining questions that models can answer from the annotated evidence but cannot resolve using recent observations, post-evidence observations, or textual input alone. This helps disentangle memory limitations from understanding failures while reducing memory-independent shortcuts. MemInStream provides a systematic evaluation of memory capabilities through four major categories, covering Episodic Retrieval, State Tracking \& Updating, Multi-Evidence Integration, and Contextual Memory across diverse video scenarios. Overall, \textsc{MemInStream} provides a comprehensive and reliable benchmark for systematically evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [75] [Ultrasound Elastography: Excitation Methods and Low-Cost Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15645)
*Corin F. Otesteanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了通过智能手机作为机械激励源的低成像成本超声弹性成像方法，并展示了其与专用执行器产生的弹性重建结果相似，从而证明了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弹性成像方法通常依赖于专用设备和工具，成本高昂并复杂。此研究意在通过智能手机简化和降低超声弹性成像的成本。

Method: 使用智能手机作为机械激励，结合有限元方法（FEM）来解决弹性逆问题，从而实现弹性图像重建。

Result: 研究对模拟组织的仿真进行了弹性重建，并与专用执行器产生的结果相近，显示了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了通过智能手机实现超声弹性成像的技术可行性，并展示了一条降低成本和复杂度的新途径。

Abstract: Ultrasound elastography (or elasticity imaging) is a method that characterizes the tissue mechanical properties with the purpose of aiding the diagnosis of certain pathologies. Many novel clinical applications use the assumption that these tissue properties are changed in the presence of pathological processes. Characterization of tissue mechanical properties is achieved by imaging the response of the tissue to an induced stress or excitation. A plethora of approaches have been used in the scientific community to induce excitation in tissue, which usually require additional tools or devices than are costly. Furthermore these are usually integrated in the imaging pipeline using additional software or hardware for control. An alternative that is simpler, faster to integrate (and low-cost) is proposed in this work, by using readily available smartphones as a source of mechanical excitation. The induced displacements are used to solve the elasticity inverse problem (IP) using a finite element method (FEM) approach. Preliminary elasticity reconstructions of a phantom that mimics tissue are similar to when using a dedicated actuator as means of inducing harmonic displacements, illustrating the feasibility of our low-cost ultrasound elasography approach.

</details>


### [76] [Taming Neural Fields with a Frequency-Lipschitz Curriculum for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15652)
*Songtao Hu,Jingrui Wu,Hong Ding,Fanhao Zhu,Yunhao Liu,Suyang Xi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLIC是一种基于频率- Lipschitz curriculum的自先验隐式神经表示框架，旨在通过明确定义和可调度的网络表示能力，优化稀疏视图CT的重建质量，尤其是在低剂量设置下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐式神经表示方法虽然可以用于稀疏视图CT的数据恢复，但在优化过程中难以控制高频率容量的暴露，导致重建结果容易出现条纹伪影和虚构的结构。FLIC通过提出一种可控制的频率- Lipschitz curriculum方法来解决这一问题，从而提高了稀疏视图CT重建的无监督质量。

Method: FLIC采用了一种新的隐式神经表示框架，结合了随机傅里叶特征编码和谱正规化多层感知机解码，通过非均匀Lipschitz预算引导重建沿着从粗到细的路径，稳定的大体结构到精细的细节。此外，FLIC还包括一种先验引导的自适应射线采样策略，可在高梯度解剖边界附近集中积分样本，提高前向模型的保真度。整个框架只在一个训练阶段运行，无需迭代去噪。

Result: 在四个公开的CT数据集下的无噪声和低剂量设置中进行了广泛的实验，表明FLIC方法在无监督重建质量方面达到了最先进的水平，且在各种解剖结构上具有一致的表现。

Conclusion: FLIC通过一种新的隐式神经表示框架显著提升了稀疏视图CT的重建质量，特别是在低剂量设置下，展示了在无噪声和低剂量条件下的优越性。

Abstract: Sparse-view computed tomography (SVCT) reduces radiation dose by acquiring fewer projection views, but the resulting under-determined inverse problem requires careful regularization to prevent reconstruction algorithms from fitting streak artifacts and hallucinated structures. Existing implicit neural representation (INR) methods rely on implicit spectral bias or iterative external denoising, yet they provide limited control over when high-frequency capacity is exposed during optimization. To address this limitation, we propose FLIC (Frequency-Lipschitz INR Curriculum), a capacity-controlled self-prior INR framework that makes the network's representational capacity explicit and schedulable. The core of our method is a joint frequency-Lipschitz curriculum that couples random Fourier feature encoding with spectrally normalized MLP decoding under non-uniform Lipschitz budgets, guiding reconstruction along a coarse-to-fine trajectory from stable global anatomy to fine details. The terminal capacity is automatically calibrated from FBP gradient statistics, enabling scan-adaptive regularization without per-scan manual tuning of the terminal Lipschitz budget. We further introduce a prior-guided adaptive ray sampling strategy that concentrates integration samples near high-gradient anatomical boundaries, improving forward-model fidelity. The entire framework operates in a single training stage without iterative denoising, enabling efficient per-image reconstruction. Extensive experiments on four public CT datasets under noise-free and low-dose settings show that our method achieves state-of-the-art unsupervised reconstruction quality with consistent performance across diverse anatomical structures.

</details>


### [77] [CGOcc: Learning Cross-Gaussian Dependencies for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15692)
*Zirui Huang,Jiongle He,Yunfan Zeng,Yuanzhi Lu,Guangye Li,Boyan Tang,Weite SU,Pedro V. Sander,Amy Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGOcc是一种监督多摄像机框架，通过显式建模交叉高斯依赖性来改善3D语义占用预测，从而在高高斯重叠和边界接近处获得更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视了交叉高斯依赖性，限制了分类读出利用类别特定关系证据的能力。

Method: CGOcc框架结合了外观和几何观测，通过对角线加低秩表示编码交叉高斯依赖性。读出步骤利用这些统计信息细化占用类概率。

Result: 在nuScenes上的实验表明，CGOcc在高高斯重叠和边界附近取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 实验证明，Covariance-aware预测与几何交互具有良好的协同作用。

Abstract: In Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction, overlapping primitives can provide ambiguous class predictions, making their joint interpretation important for voxel classification. However, current methods using spatially weighted mixtures neglect explicit modeling of cross-primitive dependencies, limiting the readout's ability to utilize category-specific relational evidence. Hence, we introduce CGOcc, a supervised multi-camera framework that explicitly models Cross-Gaussian dependencies for 3D semantic Occupancy prediction. CGOcc integrates appearance and geometric observations to refine Gaussian states and encodes cross-Gaussian dependencies in a diagonal-plus-low-rank representation. To incorporate these dependencies into voxel classification, CGOcc uses a class-conditional projection to extract diagonal and signed cross-Gaussian statistics without constructing a dense dependency matrix. The readout then refines occupied-class probabilities using these statistics while preserving a learned reference's occupied mass and free probability. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate CGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with larger gains at high Gaussian overlap and improved predictions near boundaries. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of covariance-aware prediction and support its synergy with geometric interaction.

</details>


### [78] [How Far Can Frozen Vision-Language Models Go on Event Data? A Training-Free Study on EventBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15756)
*Md Kamrujjaman Mobin,Md Salman Shamil,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该系统通过对五类事件摄像理解任务（手势识别、详细理解、因果推理、事件OCR和动作识别）中事件数据的表示和提示，实现了62.28%的整体准确率，尽管没有使用挑战的数据集，并且在详细理解和因果推理任务中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在评估RGB预训练模型在事件数据上的表现，不依赖于挑战提供的训练数据，通过不同的数据表示和提示来测试模型的泛化能力。

Method: 系统采用了对于四类任务（手势识别、详细理解、因果推理、事件OCR）的框渲染方法，将选定的事件框渲染为红/ON和蓝/OFF帧，然后拼接成一张网格图像，并使用特定类别提示查询同一个冻结的Qwen3.5 9B视觉语言模型；对于动作识别任务，则直接使用了组织方提供的EventGPT-Plus-2B模型。

Result: 该系统在2084个测试问题上达到了62.28%的整体准确率，其中在动作识别和因果推理任务上表现最优，分别为80.27%和77.22%；但在手势识别和事件OCR任务上表现较差，分别为54.70%和34.57%。

Conclusion: 研究团队还分析了测试数据集，指出边界框切割内容不紧密，导致事件OCR文本以大约五分之一的水平分辨率传递给模型；详细理解预测倾向于前两个答案选项。

Abstract: We present a training-free system for the EBMV @ ECCV 2026 challenge "Event Stream Understanding with EventBench" (General Understanding track), which evaluates vision-language models on five event-camera understanding tasks. We use none of the challenge's training data: our goal is to measure how far off-the-shelf, RGB-pretrained models can go on event data through representation and prompting alone. For four of the five categories (gesture recognition, detailed understanding, causal reasoning, and event OCR) we render event bins as red/ON, blue/OFF frames, select a category-specific subset of bins, stitch them into a single grid image, and query the same frozen Qwen3.5 9B vision-language model with a category-specific prompt for all four tasks. The fifth category, action recognition, feeds every bin directly into the organizers' EventGPT-Plus-2B. The system reaches 62.28% overall accuracy across 2,084 test questions (rank 9/27), led by action recognition and causal reasoning (80.27%, 77.22%) and limited most by gesture recognition and event OCR (54.70%, 34.57%). We visualize test samples from every category together with the exact images the model receives, score six alternative representations against the evaluation server, and analyze the full test set post hoc. That analysis shows that our bounding-box crop almost never tightened around content, so event OCR text reached the model at roughly one fifth of its horizontal resolution, and that detailed-understanding predictions are skewed toward the first two answer options.

</details>


### [79] [Image Deblending with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15760)
*Dan Botchan,Ohad Fried,Yacov Hel-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需训练的框架，通过文本提示引导的潜在扩散模型进行解混叠任务，该模型能够同时重建两张图像并捕捉细微细节。实验表明，该方法在未见过的领域和空间变化的 alpha 图上实现了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆问题解决方法通常针对单一输出的重建问题，而图像混叠问题涉及到复合图像的多源重建，这使得该任务成为不可约解的问题。为了克服这一挑战，作者提出了一种从人类感知启发的解混叠方法。

Method: 本文采用了一种两阶段架构。第一阶段，文本引导的潜在扩散模型采用高阶线索进行语义分离；第二阶段，对像素空间中的解混叠结果进行细化，以恢复细微的细节。利用斯蒂克蒙特采样生成多种可能的解混叠加成分，并据此进行排名。

Result: 采用该方法，实验结果表明，该方法在未见过的域和空间变化的 alpha 图上实现了最先进的结果，同时在标准的标量混合基准上与监督基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 这项工作不仅证明了扩散模型在处理图像解混叠任务中的有效性，还提出了一个无需训练的框架，能够灵活地处理不同类型混合图层问题。

Abstract: Image deblending is the task of decomposing a composite image back into its source images, which is an ill-posed problem, as a single blended image can be decomposed into multiple plausible source layers. While diffusion models are often used to solve inverse problems, they typically address single-output reconstruction problems. In this work, we formulate deblending as a joint inverse problem, employing diffusion posterior sampling to simultaneously reconstruct two source images, demonstrating that a single diffusion prior is expressive enough to disentangle two full images at once rather than just one. Drawing inspiration from human perception, where semantic understanding helps disentangle visual inconsistencies, we propose a training-free framework that leverages text prompts to constrain this solution space. We address the blind deblending task through a two-stage architecture: text-guided latent diffusion performs semantic separation using prompts as high-level cues, followed by a pixel-space refinement to recover fine details. Stochastic sampling produces multiple plausible decompositions that can be ranked without ground truth. Extensive experiments show that our training-free method achieves state-of-the-art results on unseen domains and extends to spatially-varying alpha maps, while remaining competitive with supervised baselines on standard scalar blending benchmarks.

</details>


### [80] [AIFR: Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery for Diffusion-Based Makeup and Anime Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15764)
*Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Yaxin Xiao,Mengyu Sun,Xinwei Liu,Renyang Liu,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIFR 是一种轻量级框架，通过单一中间层插入恢复模块，预测样本级别的矫正强度和空间自适应残差更新，以在冻结的FR模型中恢复身份特征，不依赖参考图像，在不重新编码的情况下直接匹配现有画廊模板，显著提升了识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FR方法在处理由美妆和动漫头像引起的外观变化时效率低下或效果不佳，AIFR旨在改进这些限制，通过在冻结的FR模型中纠正身份特征来恢复其输出嵌入，使识别过程更加高效。

Method: AIFR框架包括一个轻量级的恢复模块，该模块在单一中间层插入，用来预测样本级别的矫正强度，并进行空间自适应残差更新。该模块的训练通过融合身份目标来指导嵌入恢复，并使用身份位移得分来调节纠正强度，防止不必要的变化。在推理阶段，AIFR使用一次前向传递处理单个肖像，并生成可以直接与现有画廊模板进行匹配的恢复嵌入。

Result: 实验结果表明，AIFR在ArcFace的平均TAR从66.1%显著提高到90.2%，并且在未见过的妆容风格、样式强度和生成器上也表现出色。此外，恢复的嵌入可以直接条件化预训练的Arc2Face模型，生成具有更好身份保留的面部。

Conclusion: AIFR框架通过在冻结的FR模型中纠正身份特征，显著提升了识别性能。该方法不仅操作高效，而且在多种设置下都表现良好，展示了其在身份保留生成方面与嵌入条件下游生成的兼容性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based makeup and anime portraits are increasingly popular in online content creation (e.g., virtual makeup editing and anime avatar generation), but their altered appearances can hinder face recognition (FR) in identity-based content screening. Existing methods mainly adapt FR encoders or restore input images. The former require costly gallery re-encoding, while the latter may be incomplete or distort identity-related facial details. To address these limitations, we aim to correct identity features within a frozen FR model to recover its output embeddings in its original space. Appearance changes vary in strength and affect facial regions differently. To this end, we propose Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery (AIFR), a framework that inserts a lightweight recovery module at a single intermediate layer to predict sample-level correction strength and spatially adaptive residual updates. Module training uses fused identity targets to guide embedding recovery in the original FR space and an identity-shift score to regulate correction strength and limit unnecessary changes. At inference, AIFR processes a single portrait in one forward pass without reference images, producing recovered embeddings that can be directly matched with existing gallery templates without re-encoding. With only 1.22M trainable parameters, AIFR improves the average TAR at FAR=$10^{-4}$ from 66.1\% to 90.2\% over the original ArcFace, and further experiments show improvements across unseen makeup styles, stylization strengths, and generators. Beyond recognition, the recovered embeddings can directly condition a pretrained Arc2Face model to generate faces with improved identity preservation, showing compatibility with embedding-conditioned downstream generation.

</details>


### [81] [Prior-as-Skill: Remember Outside and Read Inside with On-Policy Self-Distillation for Self-Evolving VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15766)
*Yan Lin,Jingyu Sun,Yifei Qian,Zhengtao Yao,Yuxia Wang,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau,Zhongliang Guo,Stephen McGough*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PriorSplit 是一种在帧丢失或顺序异常时填补缺少帧的方法，通过外部保持任务顺序并在模型中进行计数来实现。实验表明 PriorSplit 在帧丢失情况下性能优于其他方法，且在不常见顺序下也能保持接近 OPSD 的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在帧丢失或顺序异常时表现欠佳，特别是在自内化过程中存储顺序的方法，在不常见顺序下表现不如 PriorSplit。因此，研究提出 PriorSplit 方法以提高视觉语言模型在这些情况下的性能。

Method: PriorSplit 方法在不进行额外训练的情况下，通过在外部分离存储任务顺序来填补缺失帧。具体而言，它通过比较相邻步骤在训练集中的先后顺序，并结合当前视图情况来决定如何回答问题。

Result: 实验结果显示，在帧丢失情况下，PriorSplit 在五种不同模型上均表现出色，超越了所有比较方法，并且在 Qwen3-VL-4B 上也接近于 OPSD 的表现。即使在不常见顺序下，PriorSplit 也能保持优异性能。

Conclusion: PriorSplit 方法通过外部存储任务顺序并在模型中计算来填补缺失帧，证明了其在多种情况下的优越性，能够显著提高视觉语言模型在帧丢失和不常见顺序下的性能。

Abstract: Self-evolving visual agents keep what they learn as skills: key frames with step notes on a task's steps and their order. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is now a popular way to train such skills into a vision-language model that matches, on its own answers, a teacher that also reads the written order. At deployment, frames often go missing or show an unusual order, as when a person or a website reorders the steps. Existing internalization methods keep any order they store in the frame-reading weights: trained on full frames, the model guesses when they are missing, and trained with frames withheld, it stores the order, so memory can override what it sees. We propose PriorSplit, which fills in missing frames from an order kept outside the model, with no training beyond OPSD. It counts which of two neighboring steps comes first in training episodes, and the model checks if both asked steps are in view: if so, it answers from the frames, and otherwise the counts answer. Extensive experiments on synthetic skills with five models and on step-tagged order questions in new episodes of seen web, cooking and egocentric tasks demonstrate that, with frames missing, PriorSplit outperforms every compared method at OPSD's training cost. With Qwen3-VL-4B on synthetic skills, it also stays close to OPSD on unusual orders, where methods that gain most by storing the order fall behind.

</details>


### [82] [VibePrevisBench: Evaluating Agentic Coding for 4D Scene Generation in Blender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15789)
*Yuzhe Chen,Muxi Diao,Lele Yang,Wenbin Xu,Xuyang Liu,Qile Su,Changyi Liu,Jiankang Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Haoyu Zhang,Hongyi Fu,Jianxiong Wang,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Yingxin Li,Bingyi Li,Xu Wang,Xuanyu Zheng,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Wentao Zhang,Jiale Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于4D场景重建的基准VibePrevisBench，其中包含242项任务，涵盖四种输入类型，并从两个评价维度进行评分。分别使用规则基场景检查和基于VLM的屏幕检查进行评估。研究了11种前沿编码代理的结果，发现多模态理解在场景和摄像机运动理解方面区分了代理。所有代理似乎已经掌握了基础的摄像机操作和布局，但在复杂请求方面失败。代理检查自己的渲染结果但无法纠正错误，因此需要更好的空间和摄像机视觉理解、明确处理复杂请求和逐项验证渲染结果与简报的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前没有针对这些条件下的视频生成模型以及背后的长期多镜头场景的基准测试，研究团队旨在填补这一空白。

Method: VibePrevisBench引入了一个基准，通过编码代理将简述转化为可执行的4D场景，并渲染代理视频。任务分为四个类别，从两个维度进行评分。使用规则基场景检查和基于VLM的屏幕检查进行评估。

Result: 共有11种代理参与了这项研究，这些代理的表现被分析。研究发现多模态理解对于理解场景和摄像机运动至关重要。所有代理似乎已经掌握了基础的摄像机操作和布局，但在复杂请求方面表现不佳。代理检查了自己的渲染结果，但未能纠正错误。

Conclusion: 多模态理解是关键；基本摄像机操作和布局已被掌握，但应对复杂请求的能力需要提高。代理需要更好的空间和摄像机视觉理解、针对复杂请求的明确检查以及逐项验证渲染结果与简述的一致性。

Abstract: Controllable real-world video generation models can condition on a rendered graybox proxy video, which fixes layout, motion and camera. Coding agents can build such videos by reconstructing a 4D scene in Blender, yet no benchmark checks both these videos and the long, multi-shot scenes behind them. We introduce VibePrevisBench, a benchmark for 4D scene reconstruction: an agent turns a brief into an executable 4D scene and renders its proxy video. Its 242 tasks span four input kinds, including scripts, photos, continuations, and reference videos. Each delivery is scored along two evaluation dimensions. Rule-based Scene Check re-runs the code and compares the exported 4D scene with hidden ground truth facts in nine dimensions. VLM-based Screen Check asks a VLM judge whether the facts that pass can be recognized in the proxy video. We analyze the evaluation results and trajectories of 11 frontier coding agents and identify three key observations. First, multimodal understanding separates the agents, especially in understanding space and camera motion. Second, all agents seem to already master basic camera work and staging, and fail at challenging requests. Third, agents inspect their own renders but fail to correct their errors. Agents thus need better visual understanding of space and camera, explicit checks for challenging requests, and item-by-item verification of renders against the brief.

</details>


### [83] [Fine-Grained Generative Hallucination Detection and Correction for Aerial Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15809)
*Debolena Basak,Somsubhra De,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种轻量级、无需参考的框架，用于自动检测和修正无约束的航空图像文本描述中的幻觉，通过利用最新的多模态大语言模型进行低计算开销的幻觉检测，并通过专门的精炼模块提高描述的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用无人机捕获的航拍影像进行如监视、灾害响应和大规模环境监测等应用时，自动生成文本描述至关重要，但目前的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在生成描述时容易产生与视觉输入不一致的内容，特别是在航拍影像中，这种问题被显著放大，而现有的幻觉检测方法又存在依赖计算成本高昂的模型和仅作二元分类的问题。

Method: 该方法利用了最新的多模态大语言模型InternVL3和Qwen2.5-VL，对生成的描述进行低计算开销的幻觉检测，并通过专门的精炼模块进行描述的修改，以提高其事实的一致性。

Result: 在Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID)上的实验表明，该框架显著降低了幻觉率，且适用于资源受限环境下的实时部署。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架提供了一种有效的解决方案，用于减轻MLLMs在生成航拍影像描述时的幻觉问题，并且保持了实时性的优势。

Abstract: Aerial imagery captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) plays a vital role in applications such as surveillance, disaster response, and large-scale environmental monitoring. Automatic generation of textual descriptions for such imagery is essential for reducing manual inspection overhead and enabling better downstream decision-making. However, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), despite their impressive captioning capabilities, are prone to hallucination, producing descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. This problem is significantly amplified in aerial imagery due to its unique challenges such as extreme viewpoint variation, small objects, class imbalance, and ambiguous visual entities. Existing hallucination detection approaches either rely on computationally expensive large external models such as GPT-4, which perform poorly on aerial domains, or treat the problem as a rigid binary classification task, ignoring the inherent uncertainty in ambiguous cases. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight, reference-free framework for hallucination detection and correction in free-form aerial image captioning. Our method leverages state-of-the-art MLLMs, specifically InternVL3 and Qwen2.5-VL, to detect hallucinated content in generated captions with minimal computational overhead. Detected hallucinations are corrected through a dedicated refinement module that improves the factual consistency of the original captions. Extensive experiments on the Labelled Illusion Dataset (LID) demonstrate that our framework significantly reduces hallucination rates while remaining practical for real-time deployment in resource-constrained environments. Code is available at: https://github.com/Debolena7/Hal_Detect.

</details>


### [84] [Where to Generate Touch: Do Image Latents Preserve Material Recognition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15834)
*Wanghao Ye,Aarosh Das,Sihan Chen,Yiting Wang,Bowei Tian,Guoheng Sun,Shwai He,Zheyu Shen,Ziyao Wang,Yexiao He,Zhaoyi Liu,Meng Liu,Yuning Zhang,Meng Feng,Ziyi Wang,Yilong Dai,Yifei Dong,Siyuan Peng,Zhenle Duan,Joshua Liu,Lang Xiong,Daeun Jung,Aditya Sarkar,Parastoo Pilevar,Xiaochuan Gong,Yancheng Zhu,Yingying Fan,Qiong Wang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过生成触觉信号扩展了机器人学习的范围，使用了从预训练图像模型重建得到的触觉生成空间，并验证了这种方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索如何通过生成触觉信号来扩展机器人学习的范围，从而记录和学习新的接触情况。

Method: 研究的方法是首先通过一个材料分类器测试能否区分真实触觉和通过编码-解码从预训练图像模型中重建的触觉信号。根据分类器的表现选择触觉生成空间，最终展示了基于选定空间的图像到触觉的生成器。

Result: 研究表明，通过SD-VAE重建的触觉信号在保持物体身份识别准确性方面表现较好，与真实触觉信号相近，并且通过这种方法可以更好地保留物体的身份信息。

Conclusion: 研究结论支持使用SD-VAE作为触觉生成空间，并展示了基于此空间的生成器，进而为扩展机器人触觉学习能力提供了可能。

Abstract: Generated touch could extend robot learning beyond recorded contacts. A tactile generator needs a latent space, and we choose one by testing whether a material classifier trained on real GelSight presses still works on encode-decode reconstructions from pretrained image models. On ObjectFolder Real, the classifier loses 6.5 to 9.7 points on reconstructions from three 16x discrete tokenizers and 0.3 points on those from the Stable Diffusion VAE (SD-VAE). The 16x tokenizers again lose far more on Touch and Go and on DIGIT object recognition (YCB-Slide). Retraining the classifier on reconstructions, with no new labels, narrows the discrete gap. SD-VAE reconstructions also keep object identity, with 76.7% object-matching accuracy against 78.9% for real touch and 67.0% for MAGVIT-v2. We therefore select SD-VAE latents and show a photo-to-touch generator trained in them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [85] [Open Vision Banana: A Fully Open Recipe for Generative Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15844)
*Arpit Jadon,Valentinos Pariza,Jona Ruthardt,Ryousuke Yamada,A. Sophia Koepke,Andrei Bursuc,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open Vision Banana (OVB) 提供了一个开放的、可复现的方法，将预训练的图像生成器转化为统一的多任务感知系统。通过实验分析确定了对于泛化最重要的设计选择，包括颜色编码、提示设计、参数调整和任务平衡。OVB 在 Cityscapes 的零样本识别上超过了 Vision Banana，并且在其他任务中取得了竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Banana 已开源但并未提供足够的细节来实现相同的性能。OVB 致力于提供一个完全开放、可复现的平台，以实现在不同任务上的卓越表现，并促进研究的透明度和可重复性。

Method: OVB 采用预训练图像生成器进行条件图像编辑，通过调整颜色编码、提示设计、参数设置和任务平衡来改进模型。实验使用 4B 和 9B 参数的预训练模型，并用 2.1M-3.8M 来自公开数据集的数据进行训练。此外，引入了一个基于生成器的感知测试时扩展方法，利用多数投票来融合多类色典下的图像分割结果。

Result: OVB 超过了 Vision Banana 在 Cityscapes 上的零样本识别性能，并与最先进的仿射不变深度估计器竞争。非零样本变体在引用和推理分割任务中接近 Vision Banana 性能，但在实例分割任务中差距较大。所有模型大都保留了基础模型的图像编辑能力。另外，OVB 提出了一种测试时缩放方法，通过多数投票融合生成器生成的图像分割结果，提高了 Cityscapes 和 COCO-Object 上的 mIoU 值。

Conclusion: OVB 提供了一个全面的平台，用于建设和优化基于图像生成器的多任务感知系统。通过详细公开了使用的模型、数据集、训练代码和其他资源，OVB 旨在促进该领域的研究和合作。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that pretrained image generators encode real-world understanding beyond image synthesis. Vision Banana exploits this by framing vision tasks as conditional image editing. However, its data, weights, code, and implementation details are not public. We present Open Vision Banana (OVB), a fully open and reproducible recipe for converting pretrained generators into unified multi-task perception systems. Our analyses identify the design choices that matter most for generalization, including color encoding, prompt design, parameter adaptation, and task balancing. These choices interact, so individually helpful choices can hurt when combined with others. Starting from 4B and 9B parameter open-weight base models and using 2.1M-3.8M training pairs from public datasets, OVB surpasses Vision Banana's reported Cityscapes zero-shot performance (73.9 vs. 69.9 mIoU) and rivals state-of-the-art affine-invariant depth estimators. Its non-zero-shot variants come within five points of Vision Banana on referring and reasoning segmentation but remain further behind on instance segmentation. All models largely retain the base model's image-editing abilities. With legend ensembling, we introduce a test-time scaling method for generator-based perception. It segments an image under several class-color legends and fuses the results by majority vote, correcting unstable color-class bindings and improving mIoU by up to 8.7 points on Cityscapes and 15.0 points on COCO-Object without retraining. We release the dataset construction and processing pipeline, the training and evaluation code, as well as the final, intermediate, and ablation checkpoints. Project website: https://openvisionbanana.github.io

</details>


### [86] [Lang-SVG: Hierarchical Image Vectorization with Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15941)
*Xi Liu,Chaoyi Zhou,Run Wang,Jiaang Li,Feng Luo,Junxiang Huang,Siyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lang-SVG 提出了一个用于图像向量化的新型方法，通过引入明确的树结构和采用多模态大型语言模型来优化向量图形的质量和结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像向量化方法主要关注像素级别的渲染保真度，但往往忽视了人类感知的对象部分层次结构，这使得手动编辑变得困难。

Method: Lang-SVG 使用了一个可以控制粒度和开放词汇表分割先验的树结构来初始化对象部分层次结构，并利用多模态大型语言模型 (MLLMs) 带有 SVG 树背景来为未标记的节点分配语义标签。通过深度自适应简化和树引导的剪枝/合并减少 SVG 节点冗余，并使用 L1-SAM 提示恢复缺失区域以提高重建保真度和结构完整性。

Result: 实验结果显示，Lang-SVG 在渲染保真度和结构质量方面都达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Lang-SVG 提出了一种新的向量化方法，通过引入明确的树结构和利用多模态大型语言模型来改善矢量图像的质量和结构完整性。

Abstract: Image vectorization reconstructs raster images as compact scalable vector graph- ics (SVG), providing a structured and editable code representation of images. However, most existing vectorization methods optimize for pixel-level render- ing fidelity, often producing SVGs that lack alignment with human-perceived object-part hierarchies, making them difficult to manipulate. To address this, we propose Lang-SVG, which introduces an explicit tree structure to organize SVG primitives with semantic labels and parent-child relationships. Lang-SVG incorporates granularity-controllable and open-vocabulary segmentation priors to initialize object-part hierarchies and leverages multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with SVG tree context to assign semantic labels to unlabeled nodes. To reduce redundancy, we introduce depth-adaptive simplification to reduce shape complexity and Tree-Guided Pruning and Merging to eliminate redundant SVG nodes. L1-SAM-Prompting further recovers missing regions to improve recon- struction fidelity and structural completeness. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, we introduce a new evaluation protocol that measures structural quality and semantic part alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Lang-SVG achieves state-of-the-art performance in both rendering fidelity and structural quality. Project website: https://xiliu8006.github.io/Lang-SVG/.

</details>


### [87] [DuoJEPA: Cross-Modal Translation Between Unaligned Self-Supervised Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15968)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Mitchell A. Klusty,Evan W. Damron,Caroline N. Leach,Samuel E. Armstrong,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoJEPA 通过两个独立的编码器生成模态的嵌入，并通过翻译层实现跨模态转换，无需直接对齐嵌入空间。翻译层的结构影响跨模态的压力吸收，其中带有残差连接的翻译层会使编码器直接对齐，而没有残差连接的翻译层则基本没有嵌入空间的对齐。该方法在不同模态数据上取得了良好的零样本学习和跨模态检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模态学习方法通常会直接对齐不同模态之间的嵌入空间，这可能会丢失模态本身的独特信息。DuoJEPA 提出了一种新的方法，通过在两个独立的编码器之间添加翻译层，实现跨模态数据的转换和学习，而不直接对齐模态的嵌入空间。

Method: 通过自我监督的 LeJEPA 目标训练每个模态的独立编码器，并使用单一的翻译层将一个编码器的嵌入转换到另一个编码器的空间，翻译层的目标是通过对比损失在目标嵌入空间内学习。训练过程中两个编码器是联合优化的。

Result: 在两种不同的模态数据集上，DuoJEPA 达到了不错的零样本学习和跨模态检索性能。对于胸部X光影像和报告数据，其零样本 AUC 达到 86.6，跨模态检索得分为 78.5。对于Sentinel-2光学图像和Sentinel-1雷达图像，随着翻译层结构的变化，模型出现了不同程度的嵌入空间对齐，但表现仍然保持良好。

Conclusion: 研究表明，跨模态转换可以在不直接对齐各自嵌入空间的情况下实现模态之间的有效信息转换和学习。残差连接对于控制跨模态转型的影响至关重要。

Abstract: Learning from paired modalities is commonly equated with aligning them: two encoders are trained until the embeddings of a pair coincide in a shared space. We study whether cross-modal retrieval and classification can instead be obtained while the two encoders' own embedding spaces remain directly unaligned. DuoJEPA trains one encoder per modality with the self-supervised LeJEPA objective and appends to each encoder a single transformer layer that translates its embedding into the other encoder's space, supervised by a contrastive objective computed entirely within that target space. No loss term compares the two own spaces directly, although the encoders are optimised jointly through the translation objective. In our experiments, the translator's residual connections determine where the cross-modal pressure is absorbed: with a residual translator the two encoders become directly aligned, whereas with a skipless (residual-free) translator a paired-cosine test detects no alignment in the final models, and at most a weak one in the ablations, at comparable cross-modal performance. The learned class-token translation is well approximated by a single scaled rotation of the source space. We evaluate DuoJEPA on two modality pairs that differ in nearly every respect. On chest radiographs and reports from CheXpert Plus, it attains a zero-shot AUC of 86.6 and a cross-modal retrieval score of 78.5, compared with 60.0 and 64.2 for our reimplementation of LeVLJEPA with the same encoders and data. On Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 radar imagery, the frozen encoders fall within the range of published Sentinel foundation models under our reproduction of the Copernicus-Bench linear-probe protocol, the translator retrieves the paired image of the other sensor among 4,000 candidates in 77-79% of cases, and a patch-level extension transfers the within-image similarity structure of the scene.

</details>


### [88] [TRACE: Trajectory Synchronization for Training-Free Resolution Extension in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15983)
*Libo Zhu,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Binglu Wang,Weisheng Dong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE是一种无需训练的生成方法，能够以较低分辨率生成高分辨率的视频内容，提高了生成的感知质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频模型多局限于低分辨率，训练高分辨率消耗高昂，而无需训练的方法在细节、场景结构和效率上仍存在问题。

Method: TRACE通过同步低分辨率和高分辨率的生成路径，在早期去噪阶段，通过预测场景内容和当前高分辨率状态计算高分辨率更新，同时使用局部窗口注意机制细化高分辨率的共享潜状态中的细节。它还检查疑似重复主体与低分辨率布局，更正选定区域及其求解历史，以保持其他区域的细节。

Result: 在各种视频生成框架和分辨率下，TRACE比最先进的无需训练方法（FreeSwim）提高了感知质量，在端到端速度上获得了2.0-2.9倍的提升。

Conclusion: TRACE提供了一种在无需训练的情况下实现高效、高分辨率视频生成的方法，显著改善了文本到视频生成中的多方面性能。

Abstract: More text-to-video models are available, but many remain limited to low resolutions. Training them for higher resolutions is costly, while existing training-free methods still struggle with fine detail, scene structure, and efficiency. We propose TRACE, a training-free method that synchronizes low- and high-resolution generation trajectories. During early denoising, TRACE aligns predicted scene content at corresponding noise levels. It computes high-resolution updates from low-resolution predictions and the current high-resolution state, without high-resolution model inference. The high-resolution trajectory retains fine-grained variation for generating new details. Local window attention then refines fine details in a shared high-resolution latent state. TRACE checks suspected duplicate subjects against the corresponding low-resolution layout and corrects selected regions and their solver history while preserving detail elsewhere. Experiments across all kinds of video generation backbones and resolutions show improved perceptual quality and text-video alignment over FreeSwim, a state-of-the-art training-free method, with 2.0-2.9x end-to-end speedups. Our code and models will be released at https://github.com/libozhu03/TRACE.

</details>


### [89] [Adaptive Distance Detection and Adaptive Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15991)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对《反恐精英：条件零》这一FPS游戏中敌人的检测进行了研究，提出了一种结合对象检测和语义分割的新型检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPS游戏中，及时准确地检测敌人对于游戏体验非常重要。因此，本文通过定制化的语义分割模型和基于YOLOv5s的对象检测模型，旨在提高检测效率和准确性。

Method: 本文首先使用了YOLOv5s进行对象检测模型的训练，然后对语义分割模型进行了定制化改进。随后对比了两种检测方法的推理时间，并提出了一种新的检测方法：自适应距离检测，以及一种新的实例分割方法：自适应实例分割，结合语义分割和全景分割。

Result: 通过实验证明了对象检测模型在速度上略胜一筹，但语义分割模型在精确识别物体形状方面表现更优。在模型复杂度增加的情况下，两种模型的推理时间差异可能会显著增大，为此提出的方法在特定场景下能够有效结合两者的优点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应检测方法有望在FPS游戏中提供更高效和准确的敌人检测性能。

Abstract: This paper is about object detection model and semantic segmentation model in an FPS game Counter Strike: Condition Zero to detect enemies. Semantic segmentation is pixel-wise detection algorithm. We customized the architecture for semantic segmentation. We used YOLOv5s to train object detection model. After training, the inference time of semantic segmentation was compared to object detection. It was observed that object detection was slightly faster and semantic segmentation is slower but better at detecting the exact shape of the object. However, we found out that this slight difference of inference can grow exponentially if the both models get more complex. To solve this, we propose a novel approach called Adaptive distance detection which combines both algorithms. We will also introduce Adaptive instance segmentation to combine semantic segmentation and panoptic segmentation.

</details>


### [90] [Predicting the Needle in a Petabyte Scale Haystack: Open-Vocabulary Event Anticipation in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16000)
*Shreelekha Revankar,Mikhail Klassen,Creon Levit,Ash Hoover,Kavita Bala,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型嵌入向量差值的方法，用于自动发现全球卫星事件数据，构建了一个包含超过21,000个地点和60多种事件类型的大规模事件数据集，并使用该数据集训练了一个全球规模的事件预见模型。该模型在变化检测、事件检索和完成时间预测方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地球成像系统无法有效地识别和预测变化，特别是规模庞大且类型繁多的全球事件。因此，该研究旨在开发一种自动化的数据引擎，无需手动标注数据就能发现全球范围内的事件，并构建一个大规模的事件数据集和模型。

Method: 论文将变化定义为在不同时间点上视觉-语言模型嵌入向量的差值。通过这种方法，可以在潜在空间中直接搜索语义变换，从而训练一种新型的全球规模事件预见模型。

Result: 该模型在变化检测上的最大F1分数达到93.5%，在预测事件检索方面比基线模型高出3.6倍，在预测完成日期方面平均误差为3天，甚至在事件发生九个月前也能提供准确的预测。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地开发了一个大规模事件数据集和一个高性能的全球事件预见模型，为地球观测和变化管理提供了重要工具和技术支持。

Abstract: Satellite constellations now image the Earth daily, capturing the lifecycle of events from deforestation to urban construction. Anticipating these events before completion enables timely intervention, yet existing systems cannot jointly identify where a change occurs, what it will become, and when it will finish across an open vocabulary. Building such a model requires diverse event data, but events are like needles in a haystack, making manual annotation at global scale infeasible. We address this problem by representing change as the vector difference between vision-language model embeddings at distinct time points. This arithmetic approach allows us to search for semantic transformations directly in the latent space, enabling (1) an automated data engine for global event discovery without manual annotation and (2) the largest global satellite event dataset to our knowledge, comprising 21,000+ locations across 64+ event types with ~2M images from PlanetScope and Sentinel-2 spanning six continents, to train (3) a novel global-scale event anticipation model. Our model detects change with a 93.5% max F1 score, outperforms baselines 3.6x on predicted event retrieval, and forecasts completion dates with a 3-day median error, even nine months before completion. Code: https://github.com/ShreelekhaR/predicting-the-needle

</details>


### [91] [Iris-Z: Scaling One Generative Model Past Specialist Geometry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16010)
*Alejandro Cañada,Chema Garabito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种统一的生成模型，能在同一架构中处理多种视觉任务，如单目几何等，并且模型在大规模训练时能超越特定任务的专家模型，同时保持了生成图像的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉任务主要依赖于特定任务的模型，而本文认为应该将精力投入到能够统一解决这些问题的通用架构上，并通过规模扩展来提升性能，而不是一直专注于特定任务的设计。

Method: 本文提出了一种名为Iris-Z的一般图像生成器，能够同时生成图像外观和几何结构。该模型使用一个单一的主干网络来去除噪声、进行图像理解和估算深度，通过一组权重实现图像生成、深度估计和图像绘画等功能。

Result: 在大规模数据和参数的支持下，Iris-Z在单目几何任务上超过了现有的专家模型，并保持了原始生成器的生成能力。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种通用架构可以在统一解决多个视觉任务的同时保持强大的生成能力，未来有潜力应用于视觉理解和生成的更广泛领域。

Abstract: Language moved from many task-specific models to one unified model: a single generative model with a single generative training task that handles all of them within the same backbone, and keeps improving with data, parameters and compute. We believe the bitter lesson applies just as much to computer vision, 3D and spatial understanding, in the end to visual intelligence: the effort should go into general architectures that solve all these tasks in a unified way, and into scaling them, rather than into task-specific designs. We test this on monocular geometry, a task that has belonged to specialised architectures for years. Iris-Z is a general image generator that produces appearance and geometry together. A single backbone denoises, reasons about and understands the image and its depth at the same time, and one set of weights generates images with their depth, estimates depth from photographs and paints images onto a given depth. At enough scale it surpasses state-of-the-art specialists on established depth benchmarks while keeping the generative abilities of the backbone it started from without specialized layers. We release two models for the community.

</details>


### [92] [Topology-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16012)
*Leonardo Vanni,Stefano Gasperini,Federico Tombari,Chiara Plizzari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoEgo是一个专为解决自举视角视频中拓扑变化问题而设计的方法和基准，它通过独立处理不同时段的表面支持，显著提升了对象重建的准确性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的4D高斯打点技术在处理具有拓扑变化的日常互动（如切割、组装、开合）时效果不佳，特别是在处理撕裂、融合、开启和封闭这类事件时会出现严重的视觉伪影。因此，提出TopoEgo以解决这个问题。

Method: Topo-4DGS方法通过将独立的刚体变换分配给交互的不同部分，并动态激活或去激活高斯分布，以适应表面上、消失或分离的变化。利用时间变化的表面支持来模型化部分级别的运动，实现对拓扑变化的有效重构。

Result: 大量的实验证明了Topo-4DGS在各种拓扑变化测试情景下优于现有的4D高斯打点技术，特别是在事件发生后的帧上。

Conclusion: TopoEgo不仅提供了一个用于评估拓扑变化的新基准，还提出了一种有效的拓扑感知重建方法，提高了自举视角视频中动态场景和对象的重建性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Topo4DGS jointly models part-level motion and time-varying surface support by assigning independent rigid transformations to interacting parts and dynamically activating or deactivating Gaussians as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 4DGS approaches across topological changes, especially on post-event frames. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [93] [Sub-actions in Action: Text-Guided Hand-Role Alignment for Sub-Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16056)
*Sadegh Rahmaniboldaji,Filip Rybansky,Quoc Vuong,Frank Guerin,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究旨在增强视觉语言模型（VLMs）区分自我中心动作中左手和右手角色的能力。为此，提出了BiScopeNet框架，通过FocusAlign和MetaLoss两个组件增强模型对文本上下文以及视觉特征的利用，同时提供了大规模的BiScopeCap-100K数据集以监督子动作检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉语言模型在对自我中心动作中左手和右手角色区分的能力上存在不足，因此需要进行改进。

Method: 提出BiScopeNet框架，包括FocusAlign和MetaLoss两个组件。FocusAlign是一种门控文本引导的注意力模块，用于选择性地将视频特征与文本上下文对齐以强调与动作相关的提示，而MetaLoss是一种遮罩的、对象加权的损失函数，优先考虑视觉锚定的术语。同时，构建了一个大规模的视频级别数据集(BiScopeCap-100K)，从现有的自我中心数据集中生成密集的描述高阶动作、双手角色和操作对象的注释，增加了空间元数据以实现定点。

Result: 在评估的视频LLM骨干网络中，BiScopeNet提高了动作、手部角色和物体识别，相比对应的监督基线表现更佳，取得了被评估配置中最强的总性能。

Conclusion: BiScopeNet框架通过引入FocusAlign和MetaLoss有效提升了视频LLM区分左右手角色的能力，并通过BiScopeCap-100K数据集的有效利用进一步验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. We introduce sub-action detection, a task that requires models to explicitly recognise the left- and right-hand roles and their manipulated objects within an ongoing high-level action. To address this challenge, we propose BiScopeNet, a framework that augments existing video-LLMs without modifying their vision encoders or language models. BiScopeNet incorporates two components: FocusAlign, a gated text-guided attention-based module that selectively aligns video features with textual context to emphasise action-relevant cues, and MetaLoss, a masked, object-weighted loss that prioritises visually grounded terms. To supervise sub-action detection, we introduce BiScopeCap-100K, a large-scale video-level dataset curated from existing egocentric datasets, providing dense captions that jointly describe high-level actions, left/right-hand roles, and manipulated objects, enriched with spatial metadata for grounding. Across the evaluated Video-LLM backbones, BiScopeNet improves action, hand-role, and object recognition over the corresponding supervised baselines, achieving the strongest aggregate performance among the evaluated configurations on BiScopeCap.

</details>


### [94] [Pothole Risk Assessment with Geometrically Grounded Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16061)
*Abdalmalek Aburaddaha,Zaid A. El Shair,Ali Al-Bustami,Samir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种五阶段模块化框架，用于对已检测的坑洞进行离线评估，结合几何测量和基于语言的模型推理，以生成更有针对性的警告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坑洞造成的车辆损坏每年达数十亿美元，现有检测器仅报告坑洞的位置，而不提供其严重程度或处理建议。本文旨在提出一个框架，结合几何测量与视觉语言模型，以更准确地评估坑洞的严重程度，并提供改善建议。

Method: 该方法分为五个阶段：透视变换，零样本SAM分割，几何特征提取，使用Gemini 2.5 Pro进行坑洞严重程度分类并复合风险评分，以及生成自然语言警告。

Result: 在3,092个坑洞（来自1,119个行车记录仪场景）上进行评估，与独立参考相比，严重性评估的一致性较低（κ值为0.139），且低于注释员之间的一致性（κ值为0.605；κ值为0.098）。几何信息的加入改变了警告，提高了高风险级别的警告并获得了更高的用户偏好。

Conclusion: 几何信息可以改善坑洞评估的性能，尤其是在严重性分类和警告生成方面。该方法适用于大规模离线基础设施评估，但其建议仅供参考而非经过验证的车辆指导。

Abstract: Potholes impose billions of dollars in annual vehicle damage, yet detectors report only where potholes are, not how severe they are or what to do. Judging severity from a single RGB image is ill-posed, and ungrounded vision-language model (VLM) assessments tend to be over-conservative. We present a five-stage modular framework for the offline assessment of already-detected potholes that combines geometric measurements with VLM reasoning: perspective transformation, zero-shot SAM segmentation, geometric feature extraction, Gemini 2.5 Pro severity classification with composite risk scoring, and natural-language warning generation. We evaluate it on 3,092 potholes in 1,119 dashcam scenes against independent references. Severity agreement with a prediction-free three-annotator panel is weak (Cohen's $κ= 0.139$, 95% CI [0.00, 0.28]; $n = 101$) and far below the annotators' agreement with one another ($κ= 0.605$); against physically measured depth on an independent RGB-D dataset, it is also weak ($κ= 0.098$), although the predicted classes preserve the order of measured depths. The severity classes are therefore perceived visual categories, not depth measurements. Geometric grounding does change the warnings: relative to an image-only baseline, it moves 26.7% of scenes out of the high-urgency tier and raises critical-tier assignments by 109% (Stuart-Maxwell $p < 10^{-15}$), and in a blind study, three raters preferred the grounded warnings 3.5:1. A six-arm prompt ablation attributes the hybrid prompt's rule adherence to the per-pothole measurements rather than to the rubric or worked examples. At USD 0.105 per scene, it suits large-scale offline infrastructure assessment; its maneuver suggestions are advisory research outputs, not validated vehicle guidance.

</details>


### [95] [Split-and-scale Latent 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16126)
*Dorian Chan,Muhammed Kocabas,Xiaoming Zhao,Oncel Tuzel,Jen-Hao Rick Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为 split-and-scale 的方法，用于在不增加训练成本的情况下，在推理时有效地扩展潜在空间的容量，从而能够捕捉更精细的细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的3D表示方法在捕捉复杂物体的细微特征方面能力有限，主要受限于其潜在空间的容量，方法难以扩展，且增加潜在变量的数量在推理阶段也未能有效利用额外的容量。

Method: split-and-scale 方法通过将3D场景表示为3D坐标的连续函数，允许在推理时应用预先训练好的分词器到场景的任意小子区域内，通过减小空间范围来分配相同的潜在容量，从而提高细节捕捉能力。

Result: 通过广泛的消融研究，证明了 split-and-scale 方法在计算、内存、潜在容量和重建质量之间的权衡具有竞争力，并支持高质量的生成模型，其3D生成器在性能上与最先进的基线相当。

Conclusion: split-and-scale 方法展示了在不增加训练成本的情况下，如何在推理时有效扩展潜在容量，从而提升了复杂物体细节捕捉能力。该方法在质量上与其他超大规模模型相当，甚至优于单张GPU内存限制的模型。

Abstract: Latent 3D representations have significantly expanded the capabilities of modern shape modeling, enabling compact encoding of 3D scenes and supporting powerful 3D generative models. Despite this progress, current representations struggle to capture the fine details of complex objects, fundamentally limited by the capacity of their latents. However, scaling latent capacity at training time is computationally expensive and often infeasible under memory limits, and naively increasing the number of latents at inference fails to make effective use of the added capacity. In this paper, we address both challenges with \textit{split-and-scale}, a method that effectively expands latent capacity at inference while keeping training cost tractable. Our key observation is that by representing 3D scenes as continuous functions of 3D coordinates, a single pretrained tokenizer can be applied at inference to arbitrarily small sub-regions of a scene, allocating the same latent capacity to a smaller spatial extent and thus capturing far higher detail. We validate this insight by conducting extensive ablations to study the trade-offs between compute, memory, latent capacity, and reconstruction quality, demonstrating our inference-time scaling method achieves quality competitive with much larger models that exceed single-GPU memory limits. We further demonstrate that split-and-scale supports high-quality generative modeling, training an autoregressive 3D generator that performs competitively with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16581)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,QingHao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 实验发现，在JRDB-Social数据集上，视力语言模型在同时训练人物动作和团队意图时的表现并不如预期，且提供行动作为输入而非目标会损害两个方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨视力语言模型在同时训练人物具体动作和团队整体意图时的效果，以验证之前假设的有效性。

Method: 通过使用Qwen3.5 4B、Qwen3.5 9B和Qwen3-VL 4B三种大小的模型，在八个不同的训练分支上进行微调，并用特定的评判规则评估每种方法。

Result: 写作每个成员的动作在前达到意图宏F1 31.0，动作写作在前达到31.2，而动作F1与仅训练动作模型相差不超过0.2。使用意图训练的方法没有提升动作的表现。提供动作作为输入而不是目标会降低两个方面的表现。

Conclusion: 该研究得出结论，视力语言模型在同时训练人物具体动作和团队整体意图时的表现不如预期，且输入方式对模型性能有重要影响。

Abstract: Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

</details>


### [97] [GOOSELoc: A Multimodal Benchmark for Ground-to-Aerial Localization in Unstructured Off-Road Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16162)
*Fabian Deuser,Peter Mortimer,Robin-Nico Kampa,Anna Bößendörfer,Mirko Mählisch,Martin Werner,Norbert Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOOSELoc 是一个新的多模态基准，用于地面到航空定位，涵盖森林、田野及乡村道路等复杂场景。该基准数据与四季的三维点云和RGB图像配对，提供了分析不同地形结构和季节差异的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交叉视角基准主要集中在城市道路场景，缺乏对自然环境和复杂地形的考虑，使得在这些环境下实际应用受限。GOOSELoc旨在填补这一空白，提供适用于无道路地形的基准数据。

Method: GOOSELoc 包含9356个场景，四季各不同，地形包括森林、田野和乡村道路，结合了地理参考的正射影像、全方位RGB图像和3D LiDAR点云数据。标签细化到铺设和未铺设路面，以便考察环境特征的差异。

Result: 通过对四种最先进的交叉视角定位基线进行适应和微调，结果表明它们在非铺设道路环境中存在显著性能下降。因此，提出了结合全方位图像和LiDAR几何的多模态基线，以提高定位性能。

Conclusion: GOOSELoc 平台提供了实验数据和基线，旨在推动交叉视角定位技术在复杂自然环境下的应用和研究。

Abstract: Cross-view pose estimation estimates a ground camera's geographic pose by matching onboard sensor data with geo-referenced overhead imagery. This is particularly relevant when GNSS is unreliable, for example under forest canopy or in urban canyons. Existing metric cross-view benchmarks, such as VIGOR and cross-view KITTI, are dominated by urban and road scenes with strong human-made structure without off-road terrain. We introduce GOOSELoc, a multimodal benchmark for ground-to-aerial localization in unstructured environments, including forests, fields, and rural tracks. GOOSELoc comprises 9,356 scenes recorded across all four seasons around Munich, Germany, pairing geo-referenced aerial orthophotos with surround-view RGB imagery and 3D LiDAR point clouds. Paved/unpaved labels enable analyzing the impact of terrain structure, while the seasonal recordings allow studying the appearance gap to mostly summer-captured orthophotos. To assess the transferability of existing methods, we adapt and fine-tune four state-of-the-art cross-view localization baselines on GOOSELoc. Despite task-specific fine-tuning, the performance degrades substantially in off-road environments. We therefore propose a multimodal baseline combining surround-view imagery and LiDAR geometry, improving localization performance and providing a stronger reference for future work. Data and baselines are released at https://gooseloc.github.io.

</details>


### [98] [SPEM: Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling for LiDAR Point Cloud Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16212)
*Bojun Liu,Yangzhi Ma,Jie Li,Dong Liu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为SPEM的扫描先验熵建模框架，直接利用激光雷达扫描规律来指导熵模型的概率分布，而无需进行坐标变换。该框架结合了多频率球形旋转位置编码和自动回归预测器，展示了在有损和无损激光雷达几何压缩方面的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扫描感知的方法通过变换编码几何体到球形或光束对齐的坐标系来利用激光雷达扫描带来的空间规律，但这会影响空间分区和量化，可能影响原始笛卡尔几何体的精确恢复。因此，作者认为采集先验应用于概率建模，而非直接确定编码表示。

Method: 本文提出了一种名为SPEM的框架，通过在熵模型中直接利用扫描规律，同时引入一种多频率球形旋转位置编码，这种编码适用于骨干自注意力和解码上下文检索。此外，基于此编码，开发了一个能够有效利用先前解码区域占用信息的自动回归预测器。

Result: 在实验中，该框架在有损和无损激光雷达几何压缩上的性能超越了现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过利用扫描先验模型直接指导熵模型，展示了激光雷达几何压缩上的有效性，为未来的激光雷达点云数据处理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds exhibit scan-induced spatial regularities that are informative for geometry compression. Existing scan-aware approaches often exploit these regularities by transforming coded geometry into spherical or beam-aligned coordinate systems. However, this couples acquisition priors with geometry representation, altering spatial partitioning and quantization, and potentially preventing exact recovery of the original Cartesian geometry. We argue that acquisition priors should guide probability modeling rather than dictate the coding representation. We propose SPEM, a Scan-Prior Entropy Modeling framework that exploits LiDAR scanning regularities within the entropy model without requiring coordinate transformation. We introduce a multi-frequency spherical rotary positional encoding that captures scan-aligned spatial relationships in both backbone self-attention and decoded-context retrieval. Building upon this encoding, we develop a decoded-memory autoregressive predictor that efficiently exploits previously decoded occupancy information through reusable key/value caches. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both lossy and lossless LiDAR geometry compression, highlighting the effectiveness of scan-prior modeling across different coordinate representations.

</details>


### [99] [WorldDraft: If Code Builds the World, Can Video Models Just Render It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16217)
*Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Harold Haodong Chen,Weilin Ruan,Chenfei Liao,Liuzhou Zhang,Disen Lan,Xiaogang Xu,Qi Chen,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为WorldDraft的新基准，包含432个严格配对的测试案例，用于评估多模态编码代理在4D世界构建与视觉生成之间的操作紧张关系，提出了WorldDraft-Primer作为克服这些瓶颈的插件型视觉提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文中为克服4D世界构建与视觉生成之间的复杂关系，提出了WorldDraft基准，基于严格的配对测试案例评估不同模型，并进一步探讨了视觉提示策略来改善视觉生成的两个关键目标。

Method: 研究使用了一个名为WorldDraft的基准，该基准包含来自48个可执行的4D世界家族的432个严格配对的测试案例。实验设计通过将场景概要的细致程度与视觉一致性进行因子设计交叉，在相同动力学条件下评估各种模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，世界保真度和视觉实现之间存在显著的操作紧张关系；更加丰富的场景概要虽然强化了结构，但反而降低了外观保真度；并且，来自不同源的冲突线索会引起严重的源头泄露，削弱了跨模态的分离。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种插件型的视觉提示策略WorldDraft-Primer，能够解决上述问题，并为程序化4D模拟与视觉生成的融合提供基础，推动物理基础的生成渲染技术发展。

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents decouple 4D world construction from visual generation, repurposing video models from world generators toward generative renderers. This division of labor raises a fundamental question: if code builds the world, can video models just render it? A reliable generative renderer hinges on two complementary criteria: (i) world preservation, retaining prescribed 4D kinematics and camera paths; and (ii) visual realization, persistently binding text-specified target attributes over time. To evaluate this capability, we introduce WorldDraft, a benchmark of $432$ strictly paired test cases across $48$ executable 4D world families spanning six dynamic challenge categories. Under a $3 \times 3$ factorial design, WorldDraft orthogonally crosses draft granularity (abstract proxy to previs) and appearance alignment (neutral to conflicting) under identical dynamics. Extensive evaluations across $12$ representative models reveal that: (I) world preservation and visual realization remain in stark operational tension; (II) richer draft scaffolding anchors structure but paradoxically impairs appearance fidelity; and (III) conflicting source cues trigger severe source leakage (up to $64\%$), exposing fragile cross-modal disentanglement. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose WorldDraft-Primer, a plug-and-play visual prompting strategy that consistently improves both objectives across frozen video renderers. Together, WorldDraft and WorldDraft-Primer lay a foundation for bridging programmatic 4D simulation with video generation, advancing physically grounded generative rendering.

</details>


### [100] [Disentangled Embedding Optimization for Diffusion-Based Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16224)
*Hongcheng Li,Yucan Zhou,Yibin Wang,Xiaoyan Gu,Bo Li,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Disentangled Embedding Optimization (DEO) 的框架，通过优化可训练嵌入来匹配原始数据分布的高斯统计，并引入多样性和聚类正则化来提高类内变异性，从而生成比现有基于聚类的扩散方法更具代表性和多样性的压缩数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于聚类的扩散模型方法存在类别内聚在一起导致过度相似的现象，DEO旨在通过可训练嵌入来克服这一问题。

Method: DEO框架首先优化可训练嵌入以匹配原始数据分布的高斯统计（即均值和协方差），然后使用一个多样性的正则化器来鼓励嵌入间的分离，从而提高类内变异性。

Result: DEO在多个数据集上生成了比现有基于聚类的扩散方法更具代表性和多样性的压缩数据，展示了最先进的性能和更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DEO提供了一种改进的候选方法，可以作为用于压缩大数据集的扩散模型技术的新解决方案。

Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to compress large-scale datasets into compact synthetic subsets that enable efficient model training with comparable performance to the original data. Recent advances show that diffusion models are highly effective for this task due to their strong generative capability. Existing diffusion-based approaches typically perform class-wise clustering and use the obtained cluster prototypes as diffusion inputs to generate distilled samples. However, the uniformly distributed original samples in a category inevitably lead to entanglement near cluster boundaries, pulling the cluster prototypes closer and reducing the inter-cluster separation. Consequently, the diffusion model receives overly similar latent inputs, resulting in synthetic data with limited diversity. To address this issue, we propose Disentangled Embedding Optimization (DEO), a simple yet effective framework that replaces static clustering with trainable embeddings. These embeddings are optimized to match the Gaussian statistics (i.e., mean and covariance) of the original data distribution, while a diversity regularizer is designed to encourage separation among these embeddings to improve the intra-class variability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed DEO can generate more diversified and representative distilled data than existing cluster-based diffusion methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and superior generalization across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [101] [ATune: Unsupervised Photo Retouching Through Image-Editor Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16236)
*Divyanshu Goyal,Gabriel Ruiz,Amir Meisami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为ATune的无监督照片修复模型，该模型通过模拟相机损坏和无序编辑来训练，不需要专家编辑的注释，能够在多个评价标准下达到与监督模型相近的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准数据集存在专家编辑样本数量稀少（仅16,161对），且自然照片质量更高、数量更多（数百万张）的问题，因此尝试从普通照片中学习修复和提升技巧。

Method: ATune模型通过将照片损坏成更差和更好的版本来学习如何调整亮度、对比度等13个属性以提升照片质量。ATune设置了目标值，然后使用简单的搜索调整照片编辑器中的滑块，直到达到这些目标值。每个编辑都是一个滑块值列表，供人工调整，并且可以驱动任何实现特定更改的照片编辑器。

Result: ATune模型在大规模照片数据上进行训练，其结果在多个质量和用户体验指标上表现优异，甚至在与GPT-6 Astra和NeurOP、AdaInt等监督模型进行比较时也表现出了竞争力。

Conclusion: 不依赖专家编辑数据，ATune在多个评价标准下证明了其修复和提升照片质量的能力，显示出无监督方法在照片编辑领域的潜力。

Abstract: Photo retouching models are trained on pairs of original and retouched photographs, usually edited by experts. Expert edits are costly, so the two main public sets, FiveK and PPR10K, hold only 16,161 originals. Good photographs on their own are far easier to find: commercial stock collections and free ones such as Pixabay and Unsplash hold millions. We learn retouching from them alone: we damage each photograph the way a camera or a careless edit would, which gives a worse and a better version of the same photograph. From these pairs, our model, ATune, learns how 13 measured attributes, such as brightness, contrast and sharpness, should change to raise a photograph's quality. The edit is declarative: ATune sets target values for the attributes, and a simple search adjusts the sliders of an out-of-the-box photo editor until the photograph reaches them. Every edit is a list of slider values a person can read and adjust. The same model can drive any editor that makes those changes; we test RawTherapee and darktable. We train on 10M photographs from a commercial stock collection and test on FiveK, PPR10K and SPAQ. Nine quality judges score every edit, and in a user study 157 human raters made 9,253 choices between our edit and another method's edit of the same photograph. When Claude Opus 5 and GPT-6 Astra edit the same photographs in the same editor, raters prefer our edits to GPT-6 Astra's (54.3%) and rate them even with Claude's (50.5%). We also compare with supervised retouchers on their home ground: NeurOP on FiveK and AdaInt on PPR10K, each trained on that set's own expert edits. Raters chose our edits in 46.3% of those comparisons. ATune, a 54M-parameter model trained without a single expert edit, now matches frontier models at setting an editor's sliders.

</details>


### [102] [Reconstruct What You Can: Distractor-free 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16259)
*Joel Julin,Liuyue Xie,Ananya Bal,Mose Sakashita,Koichiro Niinuma,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCan-GS 是一种4D高斯布点框架，通过学习变形场的雅可比变形敏感性得分来分离一致的场景内容和不一致的观测。这种方法能有效去除动态遮挡物、光度伪影以及随时间变化的烟雾或雾气。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有的方法要么通过静态场景假设抑制了有效的运动，要么试图联合建模所有观测结果，导致鬼影和不稳定几何结构。ReCan-GS 的目标是提出一种方法来解决这些问题。

Method: ReCan-GS 通过学习变形场的雅可比变形敏感性得分来对观测进行分类，将动态的核心高斯和每个视图的干扰高斯分开。这种方法使用得分作为重建不确定性的经验代理，并在此基础上进行后期修剪。

Result: 实验表明，ReCan-GS 在合成和真实动态场景以及合成烟雾/雾场景中表现出色，能够去除各种干扰模式，包括动态遮挡物、光度伪影以及随时间变化的烟雾或雾气，无需使用掩码、预训练分割器或介质特定监督。

Conclusion: 这种4D高斯布点方法能够有效应对各种干扰，提高动态场景重建的准确性，为动态场景重建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic reconstruction from casual video must preserve valid subject motion while suppressing transient occluders, shadows, and volumetric effects. Existing methods either suppress valid motion through static-scene assumptions or model all observations jointly, producing ghosting and unstable geometry. We introduce ReCan-GS, a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework that separates coherent scene content from inconsistent observations. It derives a Jacobian-based deformation-sensitivity score from the learned deformation field, using it as an empirical proxy for reconstruction ambiguity. The score guides separation into dynamic Core Gaussians and per-view Distractor Gaussians, or post-hoc pruning of sensitive Core Gaussians. The same criterion supports removal of spatial occluders, photometric artifacts, and evolving smoke or fog without masks, pretrained segmenters, or medium-specific supervision. Experiments on synthetic and real dynamic scenes and synthetic smoke/fog scenes demonstrate its usefulness across the evaluated distractor regimes.

</details>


### [103] [FlowIR: Depth-driven Rectified Flow for Resolving Thermal Ambiguity in Infrared Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16269)
*Xingyue Zhu,Qinyun Mei,Zhexi Min,Yang Zou,Xingyuan Li,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowIR，一种深度驱动的流校正框架，通过注入3D几何线索解决低分辨率IR图像中的热模糊问题，生成清晰分离的高分辨率图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法在低分辨率红外图像中模糊区域生成虚假结构，导致热漂移、边界合并和退化不稳。FlowIR通过在生成过程中引入3D几何线索来解决热模糊问题。

Method: FlowIR通过伪3D深度提升和深度引导的3D卷积分离重叠前景物体，通过聚类内注意力减轻跨源特征污染，进一步通过热几何一致性损失在细化过程中加强几何对齐。

Result: 基于轻量级的流校正骨干网络，FlowIR在数步内恢复红外图像，生成清晰分离、退化稳健、几何真实的高分辨率结果。

Conclusion: 实验结果显示，FlowIR在多种退化条件下均表现出色，能够显著提高红外图像的质量。

Abstract: Distinguishing different heat sources in low-resolution infrared (IR) images is inherently difficult, as multiple thermal emitters often collapse into ambiguous and overlapping responses. Existing super-resolution methods hallucinate structures from such ambiguous 2D evidence, leading to thermal drift, boundary merging, and unreliable restoration under infrared degradations. We propose FlowIR, a depth-driven rectified flow framework that resolves thermal ambiguity by injecting 3D geometric cues into the generation process. Pseudo-3D depth lifting and depth-guided 3D convolutions disentangle overlapping foreground objects, while cluster-wise inter-attention suppresses cross-source feature contamination. A Thermal-Geometry Consistency Loss further enforces geometric alignment during refinement. Built upon a lightweight rectified flow backbone, FlowIR restores IR images in only a few steps, producing sharply separated, degradation-robust, and geometrically faithful high-resolution results.

</details>


### [104] [DEDCA: Test-Time Adaptation for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16307)
*Hao Tan,Qimin Zhang,Lihuang Fang,Kebing Jin,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEDCA 提出了一种双证据不一致性约束适应框架，通过结合基于 CLIP 的语义证据和搅乱熵及残差统计提取的跟踪证据，以及引入基于可靠性的门控进行样本特异性自适应融合，无需目标标签即可调整检测器以适应目标域的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界环境中的AI图像检测器面临着在未见过的生成器产生的测试样本中存在挑战，尤其是当测试样本来自于不同于源训练分布的生成器时。现有检测器通常依赖单一类型的证据，当对应的线索变得不可靠时，可能会变得脆弱。此外，将检测器适应新出现的生成器通常需要目标标签或重新训练，这在成本和实用性上都是不理想的。

Method: DEDCA 框架通过结合基于 CLIP 的语义证据和从搅乱熵及残差统计中提取的跟踪证据来构建双证据检测器。引入了基于可靠性的门控进行样本特异性自适应融合，利用两个证据流之间的不一致性作为未标记适应信号，而不依赖于目标标签或盲目信任单一证据流。

Result: 实验结果显示，与现有的最佳AI生成图像检测器和测试时适应方法相比，DEDCA 在 GenImage 基准上的平均准确率达到了 92.0%。

Conclusion: DEDCA 提供了一种有效的实时适应框架，可以提高对未见过的生成器的推断鲁棒性，并通过未标记适应信号实现目标域的变化调整，无需目标标签。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors face significant challenges when deployed in real-world environments, particularly when test samples are produced by unseen generators that deviate from the source training distribution. Existing detectors often rely on a single type of evidence, such as semantic representations or low-level generation traces, making them vulnerable when the corresponding cue becomes unreliable. Moreover, adapting detectors to newly emerging generators usually requires target labels or retraining, which is costly and impractical. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Evidence Disagreement-Constrained Adaptation (DEDCA), a test-time adaptation framework for generalized AI-generated image detection. DEDCA constructs a dual-evidence detector by combining CLIP-based semantic evidence with trace evidence extracted from shuffled entropy and residual statistics, and introduces a reliability-aware gate to perform sample-specific adaptive fusion. Our key idea is to exploit the disagreement between the two evidence streams as an unlabeled adaptation signal, enabling the detector to adjust to target-domain shifts without relying on target labels or blindly trusting a single prediction stream. Experiments show that DEDCA achieves superior performance on the GenImage benchmark by outperforming state-of-the-art AI-generated image detectors and test-time adaptation methods with 92.0\% average accuracy.

</details>


### [105] [Selective Generative Restoration for Video Deraining via Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16310)
*Yi'ang Chen,Han Li,Laibin Chang,Hui Liu,Kui Jiang,Junjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个名叫Selective Generative Video Deraining (SGVD)的视频除雨方法，通过不确定性驱动的生成恢复来处理视频除雨任务中的随机异质性，维持观察保真度并激活生成推理，从而获得更好的视觉质量和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频除雨方法在处理雨滴引起的模糊和遮挡时较为粗糙，而SGVD通过模型的不确定性自适应激活生成先验，从而实现了更精确的除雨效果。

Method: SGVD采用了一种不确定性驱动的生成恢复方法，通过估计区域级别的恢复不确定性，确定观察保真度与生成推理之间的边界。具体来说，SGVD利用了两个一阶扩散预测之间的随机多样性来估计不确定性，同时提出了锚点引导的时间一致性机制来规范帧间扩散演化，减少时间生成漂移，增强视频连贯性。

Result: 实验表明，SGVD在还原性能、感知质量和时间一致性方面优于现有的视频除雨方法。

Conclusion: SGVD通过自适应地激活生成先验，实现了更精确的视频除雨效果。该方法为未来处理视频中复杂的退化问题提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Video deraining is challenging due to rain streaks, occlusions, and atmospheric effects. Existing methods mainly follow a deterministic restoration paradigm, assuming that clean scenes can be uniquely recovered from rainy observations. However, rain degradation is spatially heterogeneous. Reliable regions require faithful reconstruction, whereas severely occluded regions contain ambiguous content that require stronger prior-driven inference.
