### [246] [Auditing AI peer reviewers: a benchmark on real scientific papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17052)
*Íñigo Zubeldia,Boris Bolliet,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Pablo Villanueva-Domingo,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基准来评估基于大语言模型的同行评审系统，并引入了一个多代理评审器Skepthical来验证引用支持的声明。基准被应用于两个学科领域的15篇论文，涉及六种不同的评审系统，发现模型选择对检测率影响最大，而工具和技术在模型无法提供帮助时可以提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于目前大语言模型（LLM）已被广泛应用于生产规模的同行评审中，但对其评估仍然有限，研究者希望通过创建一个基准测试来更好地了解这些系统的表现，尤其是当它们用于不同学科的实际论文评审时。

Method: 研究者创建了一个基准测试，将客观的、可定位的错误（包括一般问题、数学问题、数值问题和引文问题）以三种严重程度注入到真实的论文中，并通过错误类型和严重程度测量每个审查者的检测率以及它们在干净副本上的假阳性率。此外，他们还设计了一个多代理评审器Skepthical，用于验证引用支持的声明、数学和数值声明。

Result: 基准测试最终应用于来自天文学和机器学习领域的30篇论文，总共注入了357个错误，并且评估了十二个审查系统：六个单一LLM审查者、两种Claude Code配置、三种公共可用的审查者和Skepthical。结果显示，模型选择对检测率有最大的影响，而工具和技术在模型无法提供支持时可以发挥作用。特别是在天文学领域，Claude Code的数学和数值检测比单一Claude调用更有效，而在机器学习领域，Skepthical的引文检测率不显著低于最佳单一LLM。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，模型在检测错误率方面表现出明显的差异，选择合适的模型至关重要。工具和技术可以作为模型的补充，特别是在处理某些特定任务（如引用检索和数学验证）时。基准测试的结果被公开发布，以促进同行评审系统的进一步研究和发展。

Abstract: LLM-based peer review is now used at production scale, but its evaluation remains limited. We introduce a benchmark that injects objective, localisable errors of four types (general issues, mathematics, numerics, citations) at three severity levels into real papers and measures each reviewer's detection rate by error type and severity, together with its false-positive rate on clean copies. We also present Skepthical, a multi-agent reviewer that verifies citation-backed claims by retrieving the cited papers, mathematics with computer algebra, and numerical claims with executed code. We apply the benchmark to two corpora of 15 recent arXiv preprints each, one in astrophysics and one in machine learning (ML), injecting a total of 357 errors, and evaluate twelve reviewer systems: six single-LLM reviewers, two Claude Code configurations, three publicly available reviewers, and Skepthical. The choice of model has the largest impact on detection rate: given the same prompt, three frontier LLMs differ by about 50 percentage points in total detection on both corpora. Tools and scaffolding help when they contribute what the model cannot supply on its own. In particular, on astrophysics, Claude Code improves on a single Claude call in mathematics and numerics detection, and enforced citation retrieval gives Skepthical the highest citation error detection rate of all systems by a large margin. This citation lead, however, is not observed on ML, where the models appear to know the literature better and Skepthical's citation detection rate is statistically indistinguishable from that of the best single LLM. False-positive rates are below 10% for most systems, but vary with prompt, model, and scaffolding, with mathematics flags the main source of false positives. The evaluation pipeline, the injected errors, and the adjudication logs are released as a benchmark.

</details>


### [247] [Evaluating Pretrained Language Semantics for Structured EHR Prediction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17053)
*Ashvin Gupta,Brendan Delaney,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为tEHR的方法，通过使用先验因果语言模型并结合EHR的自然语言描述来进行长程EHR预测。实验结果显示，这种方法在四个预测任务上表现最佳，特别是在MIMIC-IV和CPRD数据集上的预测性能明显优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的EHR模型通常将临床概念视为数据集特定的标记，忽略了其自然语言的语义。研究发现通过使用先验因果语言模型可以更好地捕捉临床概念之间的关系，从而提高EHR预测的准确性。

Method: tEHR方法首先使用事件的自然语言描述来表示EHR，然后进行EHR特定的持续预训练和特定任务的区分微调。利用大型语言模型作为长期病患编码器来实现对病患的长程理解。

Result: tEHR方法在四个预测任务上表现最佳，特别是在MIMIC-IV和CPRD数据集上具有明显优势。具体来说，使用Mistral-7B-v0.3作为骨干模型的效果最佳。研究还通过消融实验验证了各个建模步骤的重要性，并且强调了时间描述在临床情境中的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究发现，先验因果语言模型和自然语言描述的结合显著提升了EHR预测的准确性，并且特定的建模步骤在提高预测性能方面起到了关键作用。

Abstract: Structured Electronic Health Records (EHR) models typically treat each clinical concept as a dataset-specific token, discarding the natural-language semantics associated with the event. As a result, relationships between concepts must largely be learned from EHR co-occurrence, without directly exploiting any prior semantic knowledge. We investigate how pretrained causal language models can be adapted for longitudinal EHR prediction by developing tEHR. Here, we represent events with their natural language description and use this text stream to apply EHR-adaptive continued pretraining, followed by task-specific discriminative fine-tuning using LoRA, utilising the LLM as a longitudinal patient-encoder. We evaluate this approach across four prediction tasks on the MIMIC-IV dataset and pancreatic cancer prediction on the CPRD dataset at 1 to 12 month horizons, with tEHR using the Mistral-7B-v0.3 backbone achieving the strongest overall performance. Ablations show that performance declines when clinical descriptions are removed or misaligned, when numerical values are omitted, and when either stage of adaptation is removed, while explicit elapsed-time descriptions provide little additional benefit. Analysis of the learned patient representations further reveals clinically coherent structure associated with distinct patterns of disease risk and clinical history.

</details>


### [248] [Discovering Effective Agent Memory Designs through Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17068)
*Xinheng Ding,Seyyed Saeid Cheshmi,Ziyang Zhang,Tairan Chen,Wenya Xie,Yu Hou,Rui Zhang,Zirui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAPE框架通过进化机制从最小种子开发出完整的记忆程序，提高了记忆程序的准确性并降低了服务成本，验证了保留原始对话和根据查询意图组成检索证据的设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探索代理记忆设计及其有效性背后的决策，引入MAPE框架来进化完整记忆程序，并通过实验验证其效果。

Method: MAPE框架采用标准化生命周期接口和迭代诊断-建议循环，从最小种子进化出完整记忆程序。

Result: 实验表明，MAPE框架在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的绝对准确度提高了12%，服务成本降低了35%，并跨不同模型和基准实现了迁移能力，特别是在保留原始对话和根据查询意图组成检索证据方面。

Conclusion: MAPE框架发现了保留原始对话和根据查询意图组成检索证据的设计模式，并展示了通过优化服务成本实现的计算分配原则，提出了代理记忆设计的实证教训。

Abstract: Program evolution provides a promising approach to discover agent memory designs and study the choices underlying their effectiveness. We introduce MAPE, a framework that evolves whole memory programs from minimal seeds through a standardized lifecycle interface and an iterative diagnosis-proposal loop. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval yield an absolute accuracy gain of up to 12% over manually designed baselines at 35% of the serving cost, with further evaluations supporting transfer across serving backbones and benchmarks. Empirical analyses and ablations identify and validate two recurring design patterns: retaining raw conversations as retrievable evidence preserves details omitted from extracted memories, while composing retrieved evidence according to query intent helps the answering model use it effectively. Transplanting the composition module into five existing memory systems improves accuracy across all five. Beyond these patterns, the search objective shapes where computation goes: optimizing serving cost alongside accuracy shifts computation from LLM-based memory construction to selective processing at query time while retaining competitive accuracy. Together, these findings offer empirical lessons for agent memory design. Code is available at https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE.

</details>


### [249] [Optimal Single-History Tokenization Disambiguation for Provably Secure Linguistic Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17091)
*Feng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Coin投影方案通过确保在前缀森林部分使用共享随机性，解决了语言加密中的停顿不确定性问题，实现了最高的熵利用率，同时保持普通抽样并优化了同步池方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如调整分布、同步整个令牌池或跟踪多个令牌历史，存在不足，无法在大词汇量下有效运作。本文致力于提出一个能处理大词汇量并充分利用停顿熵的新方案。

Method: Coin投影方案通过计算具有共享随机性的前缀森林中的前缀部分，消除错误和语义模糊带来的影响。其核心思想是通过重新设置停顿决策，使得接收方能够在每个步骤中获得一个无前缀的候选集。

Result: 实验表明，Coin投影方案在英语、汉语和日语中的停顿熵分别提高了7.2%-9.0%，在所有词汇量下，比SyncPool将2.16-6.85倍的停顿熵转化为消息位，并完全恢复了所有已完成的消息。

Conclusion: Coin投影方案解决了现有方法的局限性，通过创新的方法最大程度地提高了信息隐藏效率和准确性。

Abstract: Provably secure linguistic steganography assumes that the receiver recovers the sender's tokens, but a text channel delivers only text, and ambiguous tokenizations break extraction. Existing fixes change the distribution, synchronize whole pools of tokens at a capacity that collapses with large vocabularies, or track several token histories. We prove that if extraction is exact and error-free across full-support language models, a single-history receiver is confined to a prefix-free candidate set at every step -- even if it reads the entire future text and the sender constrains later steps -- and must therefore give up the stop entropy, the entropy of deciding whether each prefix token ends there. Fixing these stop decisions on the prefix forest with shared randomness yields coin projection: a closed-form scheme that attains the bound, works with any distribution coder in one model call per token, preserves ordinary sampling, and refines SyncPool for every key. Across English, Chinese and Japanese, ambiguous text is rare (at most 11 in 1,000 tokens), yet the stop entropy at word and morpheme prefixes takes 7.2-9.0% of the entropy at every step; SyncPool's further loss comes from space and single-byte UTF-8 hubs that merge subtrees while carrying almost no stop uncertainty. Coin projection keeps 91-93% of the string entropy for the coder at every vocabulary size; with the full vocabulary, it turns 2.16-6.85 times as much entropy into message bits as SyncPool and recovers every completed message.

</details>


### [250] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations: How IRM Handles Diverse Shortcuts in Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17107)
*Paul Grunenwald,Benoît Crabbé,Guillaume Wisniewski,Pirmin Lemberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究使用因果驱动框架评估IRM在三种捷径产生机制下的表现，证明IRM可以在环境变化和标签噪声增加时有效抑制捷径，并在真实数据集上验证了其在处理现实文本捷径时的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在自然语言处理中使用IRM抑制模型学习无效捷径的可能性，特别是在不同环境下的泛化性能。

Method: 使用因果驱动框架，评估IRM在标签生成特征、共因和选择偏差三种机制下的表现。

Result: 研究表明，IRM 能有效地抑制捷径，即使在环境变化和标签噪声增加的情况下也能保持性能。同时，在更复杂的现实数据集（Bias in Bios 和 ActorCorr）中验证了IRM对于处理更真实文本捷径的有效性。

Conclusion: IRM 在抑制文本处理中的无效捷径方面展现了良好的效果，即使在复杂的现实场景下。但是，需要精心校准不变性惩罚以确保有意义的语义特征不被抑制。

Abstract: In NLP, models trained via Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) often exploit spurious correlations, such as dialectal markers, at the expense of robust linguistic features, thereby degrading their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. Invariant Risk Minimization (IRM) aims to suppress such shortcuts by identifying predictive relationships that remain stable across environments, but its practical utility for NLP remains debated. We use a causality-driven framework to evaluate IRM under three shortcut-generating mechanisms: label-generated features, confounding, and selection bias. Our results show that IRM successfully rejects shortcuts across the evaluated causal and anti-causal structures, including under limited environmental variation and increasing label noise. We then extend our analysis beyond controlled synthetic artifacts to two less constrained settings: gender-profession associations in Bias in Bios and semantically loaded actor-name shortcuts in ActorCorr. These experiments show that invariant learning can mitigate more realistic textual shortcuts, while semantically meaningful features require careful calibration of the invariance penalty.

</details>


### [251] [Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation: Differentiable Length Control for Concise Clinical Reasoning in Smaller Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17128)
*Tanzeel Abbas,Ahmad Kamran Malik,Nafees Qamar,Sana Liaqat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数高效简洁性适应（PECA）方法，通过在一个可训练的令牌预算参数中增加一个可微分的简洁性术语，使小型语言模型在医疗任务中的响应更简洁。该方法在无需偏好数据、辅助模型的情况下，仅需一个普通GPU即可实现优化训练。该方法提高了响应长度和诊断准确率之间的平衡度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在控制模型生成答案长度时依赖于偏好优化、奖励模型或专有教师，这些方法削弱了小型语言模型的可及性。PECA方法通过在监督微调目标中添加一个可微分的简洁性术语，从而使小型语言模型的响应更简洁，解决了这一问题。

Method: PECA方法通过在一个可训练的令牌预算参数中增加一个可微分的简洁性术语，使得在生成超过指定token数时，增加序列结束概率，从而控制模型的响应长度。

Result: 实验结果表明，在一个1.7亿参数模型上微调，在医疗推理数据集上，PECA方法将平均响应长度减少了72.8%，同时诊断准确率提高了3.2%。

Conclusion: PECA方法为小型语言模型在医疗任务中的响应简洁性提供了有效的解决方案，可以在普通的GPU上实现优化训练，且无需偏好数据和辅助模型，提高了响应长度和准确率之间的平衡度。

Abstract: Smaller language models (SLMs) make it feasible to deploy clinical natural language processing where large proprietary models are too costly, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning allows them to be specialised on commodity hardware. One obstacle persists after training: on medical tasks these models answer at far greater length than the clinical question requires. In clinical decision support and point-of-care use, where time is short, such verbosity is hard to act on even when the underlying reasoning is sound. Existing length-control methods depend on preference optimisation, reward models, or proprietary teachers that undercut the accessibility making SLMs attractive. We introduce Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation (PECA), which adds a single differentiable conciseness term to the QLoRA supervised fine-tuning objective. Because the token count is discrete and non-differentiable, PECA instead raises the end-of-sequence probability once generation exceeds a token budget, a smooth signal optimised during ordinary training, with no preference data, no auxiliary model, and a single commodity GPU. On a 1.7-billion-parameter model fine-tuned on the medical-o1-reasoning-SFT corpus, PECA reduces mean response length by 72.8% while improving multiple-choice diagnostic accuracy from 40.8% to 44.0%. A sweep over the conciseness strength traces an interpretable accuracy-to-length frontier for balancing brevity and diagnostic completeness.

</details>


### [252] [Parsed, Plausible, Wrong: Output Format Controls Silent Errors in Small-Model Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17169)
*Umar Ahmed,Maxim Dunaev,Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov,Ivan Khodnenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将目标编码为普通语言而不是结构化格式，可以减少模型输出的隐性错误率，并提高模型的准确性，虽然这样做可能会拒绝一部分实际但较为礼貌的订单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对细调后的解析器可能会产生格式良好但内容错误的输出而未提示的问题，研究者考虑了输出格式对模型错误率的影响。

Method: 使用PIZZA订单基准对两个子亿级模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B和LFM2.5-350M）进行了LoRA微调，对相同的目标树进行了六种无损编码，包括固定长度的不同表面形式。

Result: 单纯缩短输出长度并未减少隐性错误率，但将目标以普通语言表示却降低了错误率2.3到1.8倍，同时提高准确性。编码为普通语言的优势在于使用更少的标记数（4.1倍）仍能达到更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究展示了将目标编码为普通语言是减少细调模型隐性错误和提高效率的一种有效方法，尽管这可能会拒绝一部分真实但较为礼貌的订单请求。

Abstract: A small fine-tuned parser that emits a well-formed but wrong tree fails silently: nothing downstream can tell. We ask whether the output format, a free design choice, controls how often this happens. On the PIZZA ordering benchmark we fine-tune two sub-billion models (Qwen3.5-0.8B and LFM2.5-350M) with LoRA on six lossless encodings of the same target tree, from 44.3 down to 10.9 tokens per order, including two matched-length pairs that vary surface form at fixed length. Across 300 runs (5 training pool sizes, 5 seeds) with a hierarchical bootstrap and Holm correction fixed before the results were read, shortening alone never helps, but writing the target in ordinary words does. The word encoding is the most accurate format on both models (87.5% vs. 85.8% exact match for the native s-expression on Qwen) while emitting 4.1x fewer tokens, and it cuts the silent error rate by 2.3x on Qwen and 1.8x on LFM2, separating at every pool size. Error analysis locates much of the gain in negation. All runs also learn to refuse off-menu input (held out: 95.9-99.0% handled) and to apply in-utterance self-corrections. Strict refusal has a measured price: 2.1-10.1% of real, mostly polite, orders are refused.

</details>


### [253] [Synthesis Smuggling: Maliciously Hiding Inferred Personal Attributes in LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17280)
*Gautam Galada,Supreeth Shastri,Sagnik Ray Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新颖的威胁模型‘Synthesis Smuggling’，其中对抗性的语言模型通过明示的助手请求交互中隐晦收集用户敏感信息，并将其编码为看似无害的内容中。用户可能在分享这些内容时无意间泄露了敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模语言模型（LLM）的普及，它们在用户与模型的日常互动中可能获取大量个人信息，这些信息可以通过看似无害的内容进行传播，从而构成潜在的威胁。

Method: 通过训练两个开源模型来从对话提示中推断用户的性别和年龄段，并通过不可见字符或词汇选择编码这些预测结果，展示了用户生成的内容可能成为隐藏用户个人属性推断的渠道。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使是没有直接手段与敌人的模型，用户共享的生成内容也可作为隐秘的渠道进行个人属性推断。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在使用语言模型时需要重视其潜在的安全和隐私威胁，尤其是在缺少直接隐私泄漏机制的情况下。

Abstract: We propose Synthesis Smuggling, a novel threat model in which an adversarial LLM infers sensitive information about a user from routine assistance-seeking interactions and steganographically encodes that into innocuous-looking model-generated content. The user may then unknowingly transmit that information by sharing the generated artifact, say as an email, social-media post, or message, through ordinary communication channels. An adversary monitoring these channels can discover this information. We demonstrate this threat by training two open-weight models to infer gender and age-group labels from conversational cues and encode their predictions using invisible characters or word choices. These results show that user-shared model outputs can become a covert channel for personal inferences, even when the model itself has no direct means of contacting an adversary.

</details>


### [254] [Goal Encoding in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17297)
*Arnab Sen Sharma,Eric Todd,Sheridan Feucht,Andy Arditi,Rohit Gandikota,Natalie Shapira,Gabriele Sarti,Constanza Fierro,Lisa Schut,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）是否及如何编码目标（内部神经表征，引导行动的灵活性目标），通过多子任务的编码环境来评估，发现编码具有因果性且塑性，能跨环境迁移，提出了非递归目标追求（NRGS）模型来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型语言模型在执行任务时是否和如何运用内部目标导向机制，以及这些机制的性质和表现。

Method: 设计了一个包含多个子任务的编码环境来评估模型的目标追求行为，通过分析特定注意力头的输出来定位下一个子目标的编码，并使用因果干预验证该编码在不同环境下的应用。

Result: 发现这些编码具有因果性和可塑性，能够在不同的编程环境下触发相同的操作，模型在执行过程中的确经常重新读取目标并更新当前状态。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的非递归目标追求模型（NRGS），解释了LLMs如何在没有循环机制的情况下维持目标导向状态和持续执行任务。

Abstract: We investigate whether and how LLMs encode goals: internal neural representations that precede and guide action, and that are flexible enough to be realized through many different execution paths. We construct a coding setting with multiple sub-tasks that allows us to evaluate goal-seeking behavior and the selection of sub-goals. In this setting, we localize an encoding of the next sub-goal in the outputs of a sparse set of attention heads. The same heads can be used to decode the LM's future actions in natural language (e.g., "I'm checking if a word is a palindrome"). We verify that this encoding is causal for next action and is plastic: transplanting it into a different environment, e.g., another programming language, triggers the same operation realized with a different sequence of tokens. The LM also sustains the encoding throughout the action's execution, which is puzzling because a transformer LM without recurrence cannot carry a state forward for more tokens than it has layers left. We propose that it does not need to. Under a non-recurrent goal-seeking (NRGS) account, the LM frequently re-reads the goal from the task specification, compares it with the progress visible in the growing context, and pursues what remains. We test the predictions of this account with targeted causal interventions.

</details>


### [255] [Learning When Diffusion Language Models Should Regenerate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17320)
*Minhae Oh,Jeonghye Kim,Jinwoo Koo,Yeongoon Kim,Jimin Seo,Minsik Seol,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新方法DiVR，用于验证部分生成序列是否能正确完成。通过在模型自身轨迹的最终正确性上进行训练，DiVR 决定何时重新生成块以及保留哪种替代方案，而不改变生成器本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的脱敏方法依靠 token 置信度难以可靠地暴露错误信息，并且仅纠正孤立错误后，周围的上下文不变时会重新生成错误。

Method: DiVR 运作时，不会改变原始生成器，而是根据生成序列的最终正确性进行训练，来预测是否需要重新生成某个部分。并在需要时保留新的替代方案。

Result: 在五个推理基准测试中，使用 DiVR 的 LLaDA-8B-Base 的平均准确度从 35.3% 提升到 43.4%，成本仅为贪婪解码的 1.9-3.5 倍，优于搜索基准的 3.8-7.4 倍。并且 DiVR 还能够与后期训练（如监督微调和强化学习）结合以进一步提升表现，并且还可以推广到其他生成器，如 Dream。

Conclusion: DiVR 提供了新方法来提高语言模型生成准确性的方法，并且展示了在多种场景下的应用潜力。

Abstract: Remasking allows diffusion language models to revisit generated text, but effective correction requires knowing when and what to revise. Our diagnosis on Sudoku reveals two gaps. Hidden states contain error information that token confidence does not reliably expose, and remasking an isolated error usually reproduces it when the surrounding context remains fixed. These findings motivate DiVR , a verifier that predicts whether a partially generated sequence will reach a correct answer. Trained on the final correctness of the model's own trajectories, DiVR decides when to regenerate a block and which alternative to retain, while the generator itself stays unchanged. On five reasoning benchmarks with LLaDA-8B-Base, DiVR raises the average accuracy of the fine-tuned generator from 35.3\% to 43.4\% at $1.9$-$3.5\times$ the cost of greedy decoding, above search baselines that use $3.8$-$7.4\times$. It also complements post-training such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and generalizes to other generators, including Dream.

</details>


### [256] [ArchSpace: Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing LLM Architecture Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17332)
*Jie Wang,Tong Zhu,JiaHao Kuang,Zhe Xu,Yunkai Chai,Dehong Zheng,Yu Cheng,Tao Ji,ZhouHan Lin,Qi Zhang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ArchSpace 是一个公共平台，用于记录、审查和引用语言模型架构研究。它利用缩放定律从较小的实验预测更大规模的模型损失，并提供了初始的基准和可重用的训练食谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前工业和学术界在语言模型架构上的研究记录不充分，存在透明度不足的问题。ArchSpace旨在解决这些缺口。

Method: ArchSpace 采用了记录实验和推理、社区审查和参考后来研究的方法，同时利用缩放定律来预测更大规模的模型损失。

Result: ArchSpace 提供了 Qwen3.5 和 OLMo3 的初始基准、拟合的缩放定律，以及可重用的训练食谱。

Conclusion: ArchSpace 的目的在于扩展影响下一代模型的架构构思范围，从而使得更多有效的架构创新能够在大规模模型训练中得到应用。

Abstract: Model architecture shapes the scaling behavior, training efficiency, and inference cost of LLMs, making it a central focus of both industry and academia. In industry, teams such as those behind DeepSeek, Qwen, and Kimi routinely introduce architectural innovations, yet public reports rarely document which ideas succeeded, which were abandoned, and why. In academia, limited compute and inconsistent baselines often restrict evaluations to small-scale, single-point comparisons, making it difficult to establish whether improvements hold at larger scales or translate into broader impact. ArchSpace addresses both gaps as a public platform for Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing Architecture Researc}: each proposal is recorded with its experiments and reasoning, reviewed by the community, and referenced by later work. To evaluate ideas before costly large-scale training, ArchSpace leverages scaling laws to predict model loss at larger scales from smaller experiments, a practice widely adopted in industry. We provide initial references that make the platform usable: Qwen3.5 and OLMo3 baselines, fitted scaling laws, and a reusable training recipe. Ideas validated through this review can in turn become new baselines, and ArchSpace thereby aims to broaden the range of ideas that shape the next generation of models.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Towards Runtime-Aware Observability for Java Virtual Threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14999)
*Wenjie Ding*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的基于OS的可观察性工具，用于监测Java虚拟线程，通过绑定每个虚拟线程64字节的非堆栈踪迹缓冲区，并通过四个USDT探针将缓冲区地址发布给eBPF消费者，从而解决由于Java虚拟线程的多个虚拟线程共享物理线程而导致的标识问题。这比现有的基于JVMTI的方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OS级可观察性工具无法处理Java虚拟线程，因其多个虚拟线程共享物理线程，使得通过固定线程ID进行请求与内核事件关联的方式失效。因此，研究提出了一种新的解决方案。

Method: 研究人员通过为每个虚拟线程绑定一个固定的64字节非堆踪迹缓冲区，并通过四个USDT探针将缓冲区地址暴露给eBPF消费者，同时提供了基于缓冲区的JVMTI通道作为对比。

Result: 该方法能够准确地标识所有测试标记，避免了先前方法的不准确。在没有消费者的情况下，每次转换的成本不超过约33纳秒；而在创建仅工作负载中，额外的成本约为20%和约1.8微秒/虚拟线程。

Conclusion: 经过验证，该方法在性能方面优于现有方法，特别适用于资源消耗较大的场景。

Abstract: OS-level observability tools join application requests to kernel events through the OS thread ID, assuming that a unit of work occupies one OS thread until it completes. Java virtual threads break this assumption: many virtual threads are multiplexed onto a few carrier threads, so a stable thread ID hides a changing unit of work, and the join fails silently. Existing virtual-thread-aware channels republish context or identity on every mount and unmount, and the JVMTI-based one pays this cost for the lifetime of the JVM, whether or not an observer is attached. We bind a 64-byte off-heap trace buffer to each virtual thread, at a fixed address for its lifetime, and publish the address to eBPF consumers through four USDT probes in a patched OpenJDK; a re-implemented JVMTI channel over the same buffer serves as a controlled comparison. The four-probe design attributed all 768 test markers correctly on both the content and the identity plane, while the naive two-probe design silently misattributes trace context. With no consumer attached, publication costs at most about 33 ns per transition; the remaining dormant cost is buffer allocation, about 0.37 microseconds per virtual thread, which is negligible on transition-heavy workloads but nearly doubles the cost of a creation-only microbenchmark. The JVMTI channel costs about 20% on transition-heavy workloads and about 1.8 microseconds per virtual thread on the creation-only one. We also show that, of the two update strategies OTEP 4947 permits, only in-place rewriting guarded by the record's valid flag is sound for readers that cache the record's address, as lifecycle-event readers do.

</details>


### [258] [Instruction-Aware Transfer of GPU Implementation Crossovers: a Tensor-Product Discontinuous Galerkin Operator on GB200, H100, and A100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15032)
*Seiya Nishizawa,Yuta Kawai,Xuanzhengbo Ren*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究比较了FP64张量积间断伽辽金算子在不同硬件上的实现，发现最快路径依赖于硬件类型。提出了一种基于指令成本的预算定价方法，并通过留一GPU法验证了路径选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是针对特定计算任务（FP64张量积间断伽辽金算子）探究不同硬件架构（GB200，H100，A100）上的优化策略，特别是在高精度计算中的性能差异。

Method: 该研究使用固定数值、输入和代码的六个不同实现版本，分别在GB200，H100和A100上进行测试。通过分析不同路径的速度表现及指导预算定价方法来确定最优路径。最后，使用留一GPU法对模型进行验证。

Result: 在p = 511时，最快速路径在GB200上是融合末尾GEMM路径但比A100慢，而在H100上两路径成本相似。提出的基于指令成本的价格预算方法能准确评估不同GPU的成本。留一GPU法验证了最优路径的选择机制。

Conclusion: 该研究结论认为在进行FP64高精度计算时，选择最优路径依赖于具体的硬件架构，同时提出了一种新的基于指令成本的预算定价法，并成功验证了这一方法。

Abstract: We compare up to six implementations of an FP64 tensor-product discontinuous Galerkin operator at p = 7-1023 on GB200, H100, and A100 with fixed numerics, input, and code. The fastest path is machine dependent: at p = 511, a fused-epilogue GEMM path is 5.0% faster than a fused Tensor Core kernel on GB200 and 6.9% slower on A100. Nominal-peak scaling reproduces the A100 ranking but fails on H100, where the cost of FP64 arithmetic in the profiler-defined FP64 budget depends on the instruction. We therefore price each issued instruction at its architecture-dependent cost in that budget, using a calibration-side counter profile and one derived calibration factor per kernel, with no fit to the target. A retrospective leave-one-GPU-out evaluation, holding out target timings and counters but not the model form, transfers the p >= 511 path times with 1.9% median error at the common MMA shape, and the direction of the crossover at the H100 shape. Restricting the streaming multiprocessors of one A100, with nothing fitted, reproduces the A100-to-GB200 reversal. At roduction-relevant orders the model does not resolve the fastest fused path; the result identifies the crossover mechanism.

</details>


### [259] [LLMRunner: Closed Workload Model Performance Testing Framework for LLM Servings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15372)
*Arush Upadhyaya,Debadatta Mishra*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究提出了LLMRunner，一种用于大型语言模型（LLM）服务性能测试的工具，它可以生成包含多轮对话的负载，同时允许配置用户行为参数，并展示了CUDA统一虚拟内存（UVM）可作为键值缓存（KV-Cache）的内存分配器使用时，不会对性能造成显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM性能测试工具无法捕捉到实际的工作负载，而LLMRunner旨在通过模拟多轮对话来解决这一问题，并配置用户行为参数。

Method: LLMRunner通过实现动态负载生成技术和端到端实验计划，增强现有模型基准测试工具的测试能力。还集成了GPU和KV缓存监控功能，以及自动收集和绘制结果的子系统。

Result: LLMRunner的实验展示了，在非超分配场景下，使用CUDA UVM作为键值缓存的内存分配器，不会对系统吞吐量或延迟产生可测量的负面影响。

Conclusion: 该工具为用户提供了一个全面的性能测试框架，证实了CUDA UVM可以作为键值缓存内存分配器的可靠替代品。

Abstract: Performance testing large language model (LLM) serving engines is a crucial requirement considering the criticality of the infrastructure for deploying conversational AI at scale. Most of the benchmarking tools used to carryout performance evaluation of LLM serving do not capture realistic workload orchestration unlike closed workload performance testing infrastructure used in other domains such as web and database engines. For example, state-of-the-art LLM benchmarks for performance testing model traffic as streams of independent single-turn and stand-alone requests omitting the end-user behavior such as think-time between conversational turns. We present LLMRunner, a performance tester for LLM serving that addresses this gap by providing a framework to generate realistic load consisting of multi-turn conversations with configurable end-user parameters such as number of users in the workload loop, think-time distributions etc. Moreover, LLMRunner provides declarative matrix-sweep experiment plans, integrated GPU and KV-cache monitoring, and an automated artifact collection and plotting subsystem.
  We use LLMRunner to comparatively analyze the performance of LLM serving when NVIDIA CUDA Unified Virtual Memory (UVM) is used as a drop-in replacement memory allocator for the Key-Value cache (KV-Cache). Our test demonstrates that UVM incurs no measurable throughput or latency penalty compared to the baseline memory allocation technique for non-oversubscription scenarios.

</details>


### [260] [Towards Automated Recommendation of Steady-State Detection Algorithms for Software Performance Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16566)
*Martin Beseda,Vittorio Cortellessa,Federico Di Menna,Daniele Di Pompeo,Luca Traini*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究将稳态检测问题视为算法推荐问题，通过结合监督预测与时间序列相似行为的群组信息，提高预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同检测方法依赖于不同的假设，且可能在不同的执行配置文件上表现最佳。现有方法的通用性不佳，无法有效推荐最适合特定时间序列的稳态检测器。

Method: 研究提出了一种基于数据驱动的推荐器，通过自动提取时间序列特征，并结合监督预测和时间序列相似行为的群组信息来进行推荐。

Result: 在407个已标记的时间序列上，推荐器正确选择了更好的检测器达72.97%，比总是选用表现最佳最多的检测器高出10.32个百分点。与仅使用时间序列特征的推荐器相比，聚类信息主要提高了可解释性，而预测准确性提升不显著。该推荐器在未监督表示由标记子集或全部5860个时间序列学习时，推荐结果高度稳定。

Conclusion: 研究证明了系列特定稳态检测器推荐的可行性，并指出需要更广泛标记的数据，特别是在有利于基于变化点的检测器的时间配置文件。

Abstract: Steady-state detection is a central step in software performance benchmarking, yet different detection methods rely on different assumptions and may perform best on different execution profiles. This paper formulates steady-state detection as an algorithm-recommendation problem: given a benchmark time series, predict which of two complementary detectors is more appropriate. The first, CP-SSD, identifies steady state through changepoint-based segmentation of the execution trace, whereas the second, KB-KSSD, uses a kernel-based approach to identify stable behaviour. We propose a data-driven recommender that characterises benchmark time series using automatically extracted features and combines supervised prediction with information derived from groups of time series exhibiting similar behaviour. The approach is evaluated on 407 labelled benchmark time series for which one of the two detectors can be identified as preferable with respect to a reference steady-state index. The proposed recommender selects the better detector correctly for 72.97% of the series, compared with 62.65% when always selecting the detector that performs best most often, corresponding to an absolute improvement of 10.32 percentage points. Paired statistical testing and bootstrap confidence intervals support this improvement. A comparison with a recommender using time-series features alone shows that the cluster-derived information mainly improves interpretability rather than predictive accuracy. Recommendations also remain highly stable when the unsupervised representation is learned from either the labelled subset or the full collection of 5,860 time series. These results demonstrate the feasibility of per-series steady-state detector recommendation while highlighting the need for broader labelled data, particularly for execution profiles favouring the changepoint-based detector.

</details>


### [261] [Control-Flow Profiling for Workload Representativeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16889)
*Mahesh Madhav*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于地址键的控制流分析方法，通过对比二进制程序两次执行的加权事件分布来评估工作负载的代表性，而无需使用单一相似度得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在性能工程中，为了节省昂贵的目标执行，通常使用较低成本的代理（如减缩的数据集、迷你应用程序和训练输入）。然而，这些代理需要经过验证以确保它们能够忠实反映目标的动态执行行为。

Method: 该方法利用了基于时间有序基本块向量（BBV）递归分析及实验Valgrind工具exp-branchprof来获取五个结构化配置文件。通过比较相同二进制程序的两次执行加权事件分布，该研究报告了覆盖范围、参考事件质量覆盖、分布距离、熵衍生量和阶段结构。

Result: 该工具通过AArch64和x86-64语言的精确基准微基准测试进行了验证，并应用于SPEC CPU2017和CPU2026的训练/参考配对及七对refrate/refspeed配对。结果表明，该方法可以有效地评估不同代理的工作负载代表性，并处理代码执行中出现的不同情况。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种无需修改二进制程序就能评估性能代理工作负载代表性的新方法，并通过广泛的工作负载对验证了其有效性和实用性，这种方法有望提高性能工程的效率并促进相关技术的发展。

Abstract: Workload characterization in performance engineering often replaces expensive target executions with lower-cost proxies, including reduced datasets, miniapps, and training inputs. Such substitutions require empirical verification that the proxy faithfully exercises the target's dynamic execution behavior. This paper presents an address-keyed control-flow profiling methodology and tool suite designed to evaluate workload representativeness directly from compiled, unmodified binaries. We pair time-ordered basic-block vector (BBV) recurrence analysis with exp-branchprof, an experimental Valgrind tool that captures five orthogonal structural profile families spanning branch direction bias, four-bit global history contexts, dynamic indirect targets, function call transitions, and intraprocedural CFG edges.
  Our method compares weighted event distributions from two executions of the same binary. It reports support coverage, reference event-mass coverage, distribution distance, entropy-derived quantities, and phase structure, rather than claiming that a single similarity score certifies a proxy. We validate the tool with exact-oracle microbenchmarks across AArch64 and x86-64, covering conditional and inverted exits, calls, returns, direct and indirect jumps, and forked processes. We then apply the workflow to SPEC CPU2017 and CPU2026 train/reference pairs and seven refrate/refspeed pairs. The corpus contains both close matches and cases where a shorter execution reaches much of the same code but misses substantial target, callgraph, CFG, or BBV event mass. The accompanying artifact supports the same analysis for proprietary workload/proxy pairs without requiring raw PC-level profiles to leave the collection environment.

</details>


### [262] [Theoretical and Empirical Analysis of Sparse Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17212)
*Keshvi Tuteja,Yu-Hsiang Tsai,Gregor Olenik,Markus Götz,Achim Streit,Hartwig Anzt,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文通过分析稀疏卷积在现代加速器/边缘设备上的性能特性，提出了高效稀疏卷积的技术，以应对资源受限边缘设备上的高效推理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍了在资源受限的边缘设备上使用深度神经网络进行图像处理时面临的计算资源和内存带宽限制挑战，以及稀疏卷积可以显著降低计算和内存需求的潜在优势。

Method: 通过屋顶线分析方法，研究了3x3稀疏卷积的算术强度，并提出了一种适用于高稀疏度的高效稀疏卷积技术。

Result: 研究结果表明，3x3稀疏卷积的算术强度约为10 FLOP/字节，相较于其稠密的对应物约为96 FLOP/字节。初步结果表明，与NVIDIA A100上的稠密卷积相比，该方法可实现高达1.65倍的速度提升，在NVIDIA Jetson Nano（Tegra X1）上可实现高达1.52倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 作者认为直接卷积是一种切实可行的高效稀疏推理途径，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for image processing are increasingly deployed on resource-constrained edge devices such as drones, medical systems, and smartphones, where limited computational resources and memory bandwidth pose significant challenges for efficient inference. Pruning during training often produces highly sparse convolutional kernels, offering the potential to significantly reduce computation and memory requirements. However, exploiting this sparsity efficiently at runtime remains challenging due to irregular memory access patterns and workload imbalance across processing elements. In this work, we investigate the standard convolution with sparse weights and analyze its performance characteristics through roofline analysis, showing that a 3 x 3 sparse convolution achieves an arithmetic intensity of approximately 10 FLOP/byte compared to roughly 96 FLOP/byte for its dense counterpart. We propose techniques for an efficient sparse convolution at high sparsities. Preliminary results demonstrate that our approach achieves upto 1.65x speedup as compared to dense convolution on NVIDIA A100 and upto 1.52x on NVIDIA Jetson Nano (Tegra X1) , motivating further exploration of direct convolution as a practical path to efficient sparse inference on modern accelerators/edge devices.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [SlipStream: Enabling HBF for GPU Systems by Mitigating the Latency and SRAM Overheads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14776)
*Anish Saxena,Hritvik Taneja,Christos Kozyrakis,Aamer Jaleel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SlipStream通过软件-硬件协同设计的预取器，减少对HBF访问的需求，提高了大规模模型推理的性能，即使在硬件缓冲较小的情况下也能达到接近理想的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高带宽闪存（HBF）虽然在容量上大幅提升，但由于读取延迟高，影响了模型推理速度。因此，需要设计一种新的预取方法来适应这种情况。

Method: SlipStream通过软件和硬件的合作，追踪数据无关的LLM内核的重用模式，并利用非阻塞的STAGE指令来创建一个需求导向的生产者-消费者管道来优化数据流动。在硬件上，划分生产者和消费者角色，并在实际需求访问页面时重用跟踪记录，避免了大量内存开销。

Result: 在七个不同大小的模型上，使用40MB缓冲区的SlipStream实现了理想HBF性能的92.4%，而H3在320MB缓冲区时仅达到43.1%。即使在较小的16MB缓冲区，SlipStream的性能也仅比40MB版本差4%。SlipStream还能支持Mixture-of-Experts模型，并达到接近理想性能（40MB缓冲区时91.2%）。

Conclusion: SlipStream通过优化数据预取和内存管理，显著提高了基于HBF的大型语言模型推理的速度和效率，展现出对各种模型的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference demands ever more memory capacity for larger models and batch sizes. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) replaces the DRAM dies of HBM with NAND flash and provides up to 16x the capacity at comparable read bandwidth. However, HBF can incur a read latency of nearly 20$μ$s, 20x that of HBM, so on-demand HBF accesses slow inference by up to 12.8x. Conventional cache-line prefetchers require 20x more in-flight tracking state to cover this latency. Tensor-streaming designs, such as H3, follow virtual-address order as opposed to kernel-demand order, and therefore need hundreds of megabytes of buffering.
  We propose SlipStream, a software-hardware co-designed prefetcher that turns prefetching into a demand-paced producer-consumer pipeline. In software, SlipStream profiles the repeatable weight-page demand order of data-independent LLM kernels and inserts a staging warp that replays this order with non-blocking STAGE instructions. In hardware, the staging warp acts as the producer and the work warps as consumers. Our key insight is to retire each STAGE tracking entry only when demand first accesses the page, as opposed to when the page returns from HBF. Demand thus paces staging and bounds how far staging leads consumption. SlipStream tracks 4 KB pages instead of cache lines and allocates buffer slots only when pages return, which decouples the buffer size from outstanding traffic. Across seven dense models spanning 16-800 GB, SlipStream with a 40 MB staging buffer achieves 92.4% of the performance of an ideal HBF with HBM-like latency. H3 achieves only 43.1%, even with a 320 MB buffer. The buffer sizing of SlipStream is independent of HBF latency and workload. SlipStream with 16 MB buffer, 20x smaller than that of H3, performs within 4% of SlipStream-40MB. SlipStream also supports Mixture-of-Experts models and reaches 91.2% of ideal performance (40 MB buffer).

</details>


### [264] [Bit-Sparsity Aware Acceleration with Compact CSD Code on Generic Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15136)
*Zixuan Zhu,Xiaolong Zhou,Chundong Wang,Li Tian,Zunkai Huang,Yongxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Bit-Cigma的硬件中心架构，通过CCSD编码和分段点积技术来加速通用矩阵乘法，并展示了在性能和效率上的显著提升。与现有的Bitlet相比，Bit-Cigma在处理FP32数据时具有3.2倍的性能、6.1倍的面积效率和15.3倍的能量效率，并确保计算无误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能和通用计算对矩阵乘法的需求不断增加，高效的计算需求凸显。现有的软件驱动方法在通用计算场景中并不总是可行，因此需要一种新的硬件中心架构来加速该操作。

Method: Bit-Cigma采用CCSD编码和分段点积技术。CCSD是一种基于CSD表示的优化片上稀疏化技术。分段点积是一种多阶段指数匹配技术，适用于长浮点向量。该架构可以有效地处理浮点和量化整数数据类型。

Result: Bit-Cigma在65nm技术和1GHz下实施测试，显示了浮点和量化整数配置下的性能和效率提升。与Bitlet相比，在处理FP32数据时，Bit-Cigma实现了3.2倍的性能、6.1倍的面积效率和15.3倍的能量效率，并确保计算无误。

Conclusion: 与现有技术相比，Bit-Cigma在通用矩阵乘法加速上展示了更为优异的性能、面积效率和能量效率，为处理浮点和量化整数数据提供了新的方案。

Abstract: The ever-increasing demand for matrix multiplication in artificial intelligence (AI) and generic computing emphasizes the necessity of efficient computing power accommodating both floating-point (FP) and quantized integer (QINT). While state-of-the-art bit-sparsity-aware acceleration techniques have demonstrated impressive performance and efficiency in neural networks through software-driven methods such as pruning and quantization, these approaches are not always feasible in typical generic computing scenarios. In this paper, we propose Bit-Cigma, a hardware-centric architecture that leverages bit-sparsity to accelerate generic matrix multiplication. Bit-Cigma features (1) CCSD encoding, an optimized on-chip sparsification technique based on canonical signed digit (CSD) representation; (2) segmented dot product, a multi-stage exponent matching technique for long FP vectors; and (3) the versatility to efficiently process both FP and QINT data types. CCSD encoding halves the cost of CSD encoding while achieving optimal bit-sparsity, and segmented dot product improves both accuracy and throughput. Bit-Cigma cores are implemented using 65 nm technology at 1 GHz, demonstrating substantial gains in performance and efficiency for both FP and QINT configurations. Compared to state-of-the-art Bitlet, Bit-Cigma achieves 3.2\texttimes{} performance, 6.1\texttimes{} area efficiency, and 15.3\texttimes{} energy efficiency when processing FP32 data while ensuring zero computing error.

</details>


### [265] [AMARA: Compiler-Driven Accelerator Architecture Design for Graphical Probabilistic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15167)
*Zhan Chen,Yihan Fu,Wenshuo Yue,Daijing Shi,Chenyun Yin,Mingrui Wu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yun Liang,Bonan Yan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AMARA 是一种端到端的编译驱动加速器框架，用于在任意概率图模型上进行概率采样。它通过引入 MCMC-IR 以及架构感知的分区、调度和内存访问优化，实现了高效的大规模并行化和降低不规则图形通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬件加速器虽然提高了采样效率，但通常针对特定模型类、图形拓扑或更新计划，难以适应实际应用中的多样化工作负载。AMARA 通过统一的中间表示法和架构设计，旨在提升通用概率模型上的 MCMC 采样效率。

Method: AMARA 提出了 MCMC-IR，这是专门为概率模型设计的中间表示法，将局部因子计算与全局更新依赖分离。基于这种表示法，编译器执行架构感知的分区、调度和内存访问优化，以揭示并行性和减少不规则图通信。优化后的程序被降低到紧凑的ISA，并在带有专用计算、采样和存储支持的可编程ASIC加速器上执行。

Result: AMARA 确实在不同结构的工作负载下支持广泛离散概率模型，同时实现了高采样吞吐量和高效的硬件利用率。在不规则工作负载上，AMARA 的采样吞吐量比 JAX-GPU 基准提高了 6.1 倍（最高达 16.3 倍），并且提供了比现有最先进的加速器更广泛的编程能力。

Conclusion: AMARA 通过集成优化的硬件和编译器，为通用概率模型提供了高性能的 MCMC 采样解决方案，提升了实际应用的灵活性和效率。

Abstract: Sampling methods are fundamental primitives for training and inference on probabilistic models. However, practical deployment of sampling algorithms such as Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) remains difficult on general-purpose processors. Existing hardware accelerators improve sampling efficiency, but they are typically specialized for a narrow class of models, graph topologies, or update schedules, making it difficult to support the diverse workloads used in real applications. This paper presents AMARA, an end-to-end compiler-driven accelerator framework for probabilistic sampling on arbitrary probabilistic graphical models. AMARA introduces MCMC-IR, a domain-specific intermediate representation that separates local factorized computation from global update dependencies. Based on this representation, the compiler performs architecture-aware partitioning, scheduling, and memory-access optimization to expose parallelism and reduce irregular graph communication. The optimized program is lowered to a compact ISA and executed on a programmable ASIC accelerator with specialized compute, sampling, and memory support. By co-designing the compiler and the architecture, AMARA bridges the gap between model generality and accelerator efficiency for practical MCMC-based probabilistic computing. We evaluate AMARA across representative workloads with different structures, including grid-structured and irregular energy-based models, and causal inference networks. The results show that AMARA supports a broad spectrum of discrete probabilistic models while achieving high sampling throughput and efficient hardware utilization. AMARA achieves an average of 6.1$\times$ (up to 16.3$\times$) higher sampling throughput than a JAX-GPU baseline on irregular workloads, and offers broader programmability than state-of-the-art accelerators.

</details>


### [266] [TQIR: Trellis Quantization with Integer Reconstruction for Bandwidth-Efficient Edge LLM Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15231)
*Kuang-Ting Huang,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TQIR 提出了一种算法和硬件协同设计方法，通过解耦存储精度和解码精度，提供了更好的准确性和硬件性能贸易关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超低比特梯度量化方法无法充分解决运行时 Hadamard 变换和解码成本的问题，TQIR 提供了一种解决方案来优化这些方面。

Method: TQIR 方法包括在线下绘制梯度匹配浮点候选数，同时将每个候选数限制为 INT8 或 INT4 编码，可以在解码时精确重现，并重构为整数 GEMM。此外，TQIR 还重新定位了反向 Hadamard 变换，并使用 128 阶子空间 Hadamard 变换和一个与子空间对齐的 128x2 梯度瓷砖。

Result: TQIR 的可配置解码器在 TSMC 16 纳米工艺下每秒可以达到 307.2 Gweights，并且面积效率是 Ecco 和 Codeword Decomposition 的 13.93 倍和 1.52 倍。

Conclusion: TQIR 方法可以通过固定率梯度压缩减少权重流量并保持与评估的内存和计算单元率匹配的整数 GEMM 数据流路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference is often limited by external-memory bandwidth, while existing ultra-low-bit trellis quantization reconstructs floating-point weights and leaves runtime Hadamard-transform and decoder costs unresolved. This paper presents TQIR, an algorithm--hardware co-design that decouples storage precision ($S$) from decoded-weight ($W$) and activation ($A$) compute precisions, enabling a better accuracy--hardware trade-off. TQIR performs trellis matching with floating-point candidates offline while constraining each candidate to an INT8 or INT4 codeword that can be reproduced exactly for integer GEMM at runtime. To reduce transform cost while preserving the integer weight path, TQIR relocates the reverse Hadamard transform to the activation side and employs an $H_{128}$ Subspace Hadamard Transform, together with a subspace-aligned $128\times2$ trellis tile that preserves the original 256-weight sequence length. The configurable decoder, synthesized in a TSMC 16-nm process, delivers 307.2~Gweights/s in 0.0509~mm$^2$ and achieves 13.93$\times$ and 1.52$\times$ the normalized decoder-area efficiency of Ecco and Codeword Decomposition, respectively. These results show that fixed-rate trellis compression can reduce weight traffic while sustaining an integer-GEMM datapath matched to the evaluated memory and PE rates.

</details>


### [267] [Sub-100KB On-Device CNN Training via Dynamic Sparse Updates and Hardware--Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15236)
*I-Hsuan Li,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于动态稀疏更新机制的卷积神经网络训练加速器，通过减少激活存储和优化计算流程，实现了高效的边缘设备训练，支持在28nm TSMC工艺下FP8精度下运行，具有高的能效和面积效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实时处理和个性化深度学习的需求增加，边缘设备训练变得必要但具有挑战性，因为这些设备通常受到高内存和计算成本的限制。

Method: 研究采用了动态稀疏更新机制，结合随机通道策略选择性更新重要参数，同时与激活剪枝技术结合，减少了激活存储。硬件设计采用了融合的前向数据流，同时处理分组归一化和卷积，通过重新排序计算和有效的零跳过优化计算速度。

Result: 研究设计能够在28nm TSMC工艺下以FP8精度运行，达到800 MHz的操作频率，8 TOPS的峰值吞吐量，31.13 TOPS/W的能效和14.8 TOPS/mm²的面积效率。功耗范围从257到272 mW，在不到100KB的片上SRAM预算（95KB在硅片上）内实现了设备上训练。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法显著提高了边缘设备上卷积神经网络的训练效率，使得在资源有限的边缘设备上进行高效的深度学习训练成为可能。

Abstract: The increasing demand for real-time processing and personalized deep learning has made edge-device training essential but challenging due to high memory and computational costs. To address this, we propose a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) training accelerator with a dynamic sparse update mechanism that selectively updates important parameters using a random channel strategy, avoiding costly evaluations. Combined with activation pruning, our approach reduces activation storage to just 3.4\% of the original. Our hardware features a fused forward dataflow that concurrently processes group normalization and convolution, reducing training time by 45\% through computation reordering and efficient zero-skipping. Implemented in TSMC 28nm at FP8 precision, the design operates at 800 MHz, achieving 8 TOPS peak throughput, 31.13 TOPS/W energy efficiency, and 14.8 TOPS/mm$^2$ area efficiency, with power consumption ranging from 257 to 272 mW. The design enables on-device training within a sub-100KB on-chip SRAM budget (95KB in silicon).

</details>


### [268] [HARE: Fast Read Disturbance Threshold Profiling in Modern DRAM Chips via Reduced Timing Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15469)
*F. Nisa Bostanci,Ismail E. Yuksel,Ataberk Olgun,Harsh Songara,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,Haocong Luo,Konstantinos Marios Sgouras,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HARE 是一种在线 RDT (读取疲劳阈值) 优化技术，它通过放宽 DRAM 命令的时间约束来加速 RDT 测量过程，使得 DRAM 行的 RDT 测量比 RowHammer 方式快 31.2%，同时能有效地评估模块的最小 RDT，并保持行间的 RDT 排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 DRAM 芯片存在读取干扰问题，现有方法依赖于准确且耗时的 RDT 测量，而 HARE 旨在通过减少 DRAM 命令的时间约束快速完成 RDT 测量，提高系统性能。

Method: HARE 通过减少连续 DRAM 命令之间的时序限制来缩短 RDT 测量时间，并在低激活计数下诱发位翻转，同时也能快速广泛地测量整个 DRAM 模块的 RDT。

Result: HARE 在 180 块 DDR4 DRAM 芯片中展示了其有效性：它比 RowHammer 方法快 31.2%，对 95% 以上的细胞产生位翻转效果，并能保持行间 RDT 排序，从而能够在小范围内重建 RDT 以优化目标。

Conclusion: HARE 提供了一种有效且快速的在线 RDT 优化方法，能够显著提升系统性能并优于传统的 RowHammer 方法。

Abstract: Modern DRAM chips are vulnerable to read disturbance, which manifests as bitflips in a victim row physically near an aggressor row that is repeatedly activated. State-of-the-art mitigations rely on accurate and exhaustive profiling of the read disturbance threshold (RDT), i.e., the minimum number of aggressor row activations needed to induce the first bitflip. However, RDT changes significantly and unpredictably over time, motivating online profilers that dynamically adjust a mitigation's RDT configuration. RDT profiling is costly for online profilers (e.g., hundreds of seconds for a 16-bank DDR4 module) because 1) measuring a single DRAM row's RDT requires many activations and 2) each DRAM row can have a different RDT, requiring many rows to be profiled.
  We introduce HARE, an online RDT profiling technique that intentionally exacerbates read disturbance to quickly profile a DRAM module. HARE's key idea is to reduce the timing constraints between consecutive DRAM commands during profiling. Doing so significantly reduces profiling time by 1) directly reducing the execution time of the profiling command sequence and 2) exacerbating read disturbance such that the first bitflip occurs at a smaller activation count than with the conventional RowHammer sequence.
  From our characterization of 180 real DDR4 DRAM chips from four major DRAM manufacturers, we make three observations. First, HARE measures a DRAM row's RDT 31.2% faster than the RowHammer profiler and a DRAM module's minimum RDT with an average reduction of 21.7%. Second, HARE flips more than 95% of the cells that the RowHammer pattern flips. Third, HARE preserves the RDT ranking of rows well enough such that it can exhaustively reprofile a small set of rows to improve its minimum RDT estimation. Our end-to-end evaluations show that HARE enables significant system performance improvements and outperforms the RowHammer profiler.

</details>


### [269] [VISTA: View-Independent micro-Doppler Reconstruction with Multistatic ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16098)
*Jacopo Pegoraro,Marco Canil,Aurora Peloso,Jesus O. Lacruz,Xitao Gong,Richard A. Stirling-Gallacher,Michele Rossi,Joerg Widmer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VISTA的新方法，用于在绝对坐标框架下重构微-Doppler (uD) 谱图，使其不受ISAC节点空间分布的影响，从而实现视图无关的多普勒特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的ISAC系统具有较强的方向依赖性和视角盲点限制，而现有方法依赖于特定配置或数据驱动模型，面临配置变化适应性差或需要大量多视角数据集的问题。

Method: 将微-Doppler估计问题建模为稀疏重构问题，利用从所有ISAC接收器收集的测量值。采用修改后的正交匹配追踪（OMP）算法，迭代地在所有接收器中联合选择候选速度矢量，并独立估计每个接收器的复信道增益幅度。

Result: 研究表明，在足够的空间多样性条件下，VISTA能够在速度≤15 m/s时成功重构目标的绝对Doppler谱图，平均误差≤0.69 m/s。

Conclusion: VISTA方法能够在绝对坐标系中重建微-Doppler谱图，提供视图无关的多普勒特征，提升ISAC系统在方向变化和视角复杂环境中的适应性。

Abstract: The micro-Doppler (uD) spectrum provides detailed characteristics of target movement, making it highly valuable for applications such as target classification and human activity recognition. However, traditional Integrated Sensing And Communication (ISAC) systems are highly view-dependent and limited by blind spots and angle dependencies. Existing approaches rely on either configuration-specific solutions or data-driven models. The former results in poor generalization when device deployments or target orientations change, while the latter requires acquiring extensive multi-view datasets, which is time-consuming and not always feasible. To address these limitations, in this work we propose VISTA, a novel method for reconstructing the uD spectrogram in an absolute coordinate frame, yielding view-independent Doppler features that are invariant to the spatial distribution of ISAC nodes. We formulate the uD estimation as a sparse reconstruction problem that jointly leverages measurements from all ISAC receivers. This is solved using a modified Orthogonal Matching Pursuit (OMP) algorithm, which iteratively alternates between jointly selecting candidate velocity vectors across all receivers (RXs) and independently estimating the complex channel gain amplitudes for each RX. Our results show that, provided sufficient spatial diversity, VISTA successfully reconstructs the targets' absolute uD spectrograms with a median error \leq 0.69 m/s for speeds \leq 15 m/s.

</details>


### [270] [SBA: A Practical Memory Performance Denial-of-Service Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16189)
*Amin Mamandipoor,Connor Rudy Sullivan,Cole Ridge Strickler,Mohammad Alian,Heechul Yun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 现代云平台使用内存带宽调节来隔离共存的工作负载，而单银行攻击（SBA）利用内存带宽与实际使用之间的差距，仅消耗少量带宽就可导致严重的服务率下降，揭示了基于带宽的内存隔离机制的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云平台通过调节内存带宽来隔离共存的工作负载，但研究表明这一假设是错误的。本文提出单银行攻击（SBA），用于揭示基于带宽的内存隔离机制的局限性。

Method: 本文首先分析了现有内存隔离机制的假设，提出并实现了一种新的攻击方法——单银行攻击（SBA），通过利用内存带宽与实际使用之间的差距，实现在内存密集型任务上严重的服务率下降。

Result: 实验结果表明，单银行攻击（SBA）可以在不显著增加内存带宽消耗的情况下，导致处理器性能降低几个数量级，这揭示了基于带宽的内存隔离机制的局限性。这种局部争用还会传播到系统级别的内存控制器队列，导致整个系统的性能下降。

Conclusion: 研究提出了IQ-MemGuard，这是一种轻量级的软件带宽调节器，能够检测持续的争用并针对性地限制异常请求的注入，有效缓解单银行攻击带来的影响，而传统的硬件QoS机制在这种攻击下几乎无效。

Abstract: Modern cloud platforms use memory bandwidth regulation to isolate co-located workloads, implicitly assuming that high aggregate bandwidth consumption implies high interference. We show that this assumption is fundamentally flawed.
  We present the Single-Bank Attack (SBA), a practical memory denial-of-service attack that induces extreme cross-core slowdowns---over three orders of magnitude on memory-intensive victims (e.g., LLM decode)---while consuming less than 1 GB/s of memory bandwidth. SBA exploits the gap between peak and worst-case DRAM throughput by concentrating memory write operations on a single bank, forcing persistent row conflicts and frequent write draining, thereby collapsing the memory service rate. Across Intel and AMD systems, including DDR5- and HBM2E-based platforms, SBA consistently causes orders-of-magnitude slowdowns under realistic multi-tenant settings, revealing a fundamental limitation of bandwidth-centric memory isolation.
  We further show that this localized contention propagates system-wide: single-bank congestion saturates memory controller queues and induces back-pressure into shared cache and coherence structures, ultimately throttling memory request injection across cores. Consequently, severe interference can arise even at low aggregate bandwidth, evading existing bandwidth-based QoS mechanisms such as Intel RDT MBA.
  To address this limitation, we propose IQ-MemGuard, a lightweight, software-based memory bandwidth regulator that detects sustained contention using microarchitectural signals and selectively throttles pathological request injection. Our implementation on an Intel platform effectively mitigates SBA, whereas Intel's hardware QoS mechanism fails completely.

</details>


### [271] [SPEC CPU2026 Under the Microscope: Programs, Phases, and Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16376)
*Peijun Li,Pei-Hung Lin,Tianming Cui,Caiwen Ding,Yang Katie Zhao,Maya Gokhale*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究全面分析了SPEC CPU2026基准套件在不同级别（程序、阶段、样本）上的行为变化，并评估了SimPoint采样技术的准确性。研究结果表明，采样技术在处理频繁变化的输入时需要改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的评估方法主要关注整体运行平均值，未能展示运行过程中的行为变化，以及采样方法捕捉这些变化的能力。通过更细致地研究SPEC CPU2026在不同级别的行为，可以为计算机体系结构研究提供更深入的理解，并指导未来的研究方向。

Method: 研究采用实测硬件分析不同级别的行为，包括程序、阶段和样本。通过对不同工作负载、编译器版本和平台进行分析，揭示采样对不同输入类型的性能影响。

Result: 研究表明，仅通过更改编译器版本，可以将单个基准的IPC降低一半或增加近一倍，超过一半的输入显示出时间变化的行为，IPC在一个输入内部可以变化超过十倍。SimPoint在恒稳输入上是准确的，但在其他类型的输入上误差高达43%，通过选择基于测量的每条路径的代表性，可以将整个输入的误差控制在2.6%以内。

Conclusion: 研究提出了关于SPEC CPU2026采样策略的实用建议，帮助研究者更准确地评估新设计，同时指出需要额外模拟努力以保持采样结果的准确性。

Abstract: SPEC CPU2026, the latest generation of the SPEC CPU benchmarks, is set to become the standard benchmark suite in computer architecture research, where new designs are often evaluated through sampled simulation with tools such as SimPoint. Existing characterizations of the suite focus on whole-run averages, which reveal neither how behavior changes within a run nor how well sampling captures it. We characterize CPU2026 on real hardware at three levels: programs, phases, and samples. At the program level, we report whole-run metrics and top-down bottleneck classes for all four suites and examine how they change with workload size, compiler version, and platform, across machines with three different instruction set architectures: x86, Arm, and RISC-V. Notably, changing only the compiler version can halve or nearly double the IPC of individual benchmarks. At the phase level, we track hardware counters every 10 ms over all 90 SPECrate inputs, identify recurring patterns of time-varying behavior, and divide the inputs into three groups: steady, multi-phase, and bursty. More than half of the inputs show time-varying behavior, and IPC within a single input can vary by more than an order of magnitude. At the sample level, we validate SimPoint against hardware measurements of every interval it can select. SimPoint is accurate on steady inputs, but its error reaches 43% on the others, and changing only the random seed spreads the error of one input over a range of 58 percentage points. Keeping SimPoint's clusters but choosing each representative by measured CPI brings every input within 2.6%, so the error comes from the choice of representatives, not from the clustering. We conclude with practical recommendations on sampling strategy for CPU2026, which give researchers a deeper understanding of the suite and show where additional simulation effort is needed to keep sampled results accurate.

</details>


### [272] [An Agentic Python Framework for LLM-Driven UVM Testbench Synthesis with Closed-Loop Compiler Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16202)
*Vikash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代理Python框架，可以从结构化的验证表现（JSON规范）生成完整的协议感知UVM测试平台，大幅减少了验证工程师的启动时间，并提高了功能覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SoC开发中协议桥验证的持续瓶颈问题，减少工程师手工构建UVM测试平台的时间和工作量。

Method: 采用一种双重闭环架构的代理Python框架，通过内层编译反馈环路注入分类的UVM诊断信息，而不是原始工具输出，并通过外层意图差距分析驱动覆盖率循环。

Result: 在AMBA AHB-to-APB桥上验证，获得了96.8%的功能覆盖率，启动速度提高了4.7倍，生成的4,623行测试平台上只有2.1%需要人工修正，还独立识别出一个关键的RTL功能错误，自动化比手动方法更准确。

Conclusion: 该框架在四个协议家族中的普遍性验证中表现出更高的提速效果，证实了其在复杂协议验证中的高效性和适用性。

Abstract: Protocol bridge verification is a persistent bottleneck in SoC development: for every new bridge instance, engineers hand-craft a full UVM testbench from scratch, consuming 40--100 engineering hours of bring-up effort. We present an agentic Python framework that generates complete, protocol-aware UVM testbenches for arbitrary IP and subsystem configurations from a structured Verification Manifest---a JSON specification encoding interface topology, protocol semantics, and coverage intent. The framework employs a dual closed-loop architecture: an inner compiler feedback loop that injects categorised UVM diagnostics rather than raw tool output, and an outer coverage loop driven by intent-gap analysis against the Manifest. Validated on the AMBA AHB-to-APB bridge---a representative production protocol bridge with a 7-state control FSM, two-phase APB handshake, and non-trivial burst serialisation logic---the framework achieves 96.8% functional coverage, a $4.7\times$ bring-up speedup, and a Protocol Verification Completeness score of 0.87, with only 2.1% of the generated 4,623-line testbench requiring human correction. The generated scoreboard independently identified a critical RTL functional fault that the manual baseline missed entirely. Generalisation across four protocol families confirms that speedup increases with protocol complexity, ranging from $3.1\times$ (APB Subordinate) to $6.1\times$ (CHI Subordinate).

</details>


### [273] [Demo: Matter-Based IoMT Telemetry and Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16275)
*Muhammad Hassan,Masooda Bashir,Carl Gunter*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究展示了一个基于Matter标准的IoMT测试床，实现了心跳率、血氧饱和度和体温的模拟测量，证明了该自定义遥测模型可以在测试的Matter配置中运行，无需修改委托或安全会话机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于健康监测设备的增加，如何在临床外场景保证生理数据的保护和易访问成为挑战。传统应用和界面的差异使得不同设备的数据难以整合，研究旨在通过Matter标准解决这一问题。

Method: 研究通过在Raspberry Pi 5上实现Matter标准中的HealthVitals集群，模拟了心率、血氧饱和度和体温的遥测功能，展示了在受控丢包条件下通信的稳定性，以及通过参考控制器的实时数据传输。

Result: 研究结果表明，自定义遥测模型能够在测试的Matter配置中运行，证明基于Matter的健康遥测设备可以实现在现有安全框架下的无缝集成。

Conclusion: 该研究为健康相关的扩展提供了实际的测试平台，并支持研究者和设备开发者探索本地遥测集成，从而帮助患者和护理人员跨多个设备访问数据。

Abstract: Connected health devices increasingly support health monitoring outside clinical settings, making secure and accessible physiological data important for patients and caregivers. However, device-specific applications and interfaces complicate the integration of readings from different devices. The Matter standard provides standardized device commissioning and protected communication, but its cluster library does not represent the health attribute measurement. We present a preliminary demo study of a Matter-based IoMT testbed with a specific HealthVitals cluster. Implemented in the connectedhomeip SDK on a Raspberry~Pi~5, the cluster exposes simulated heart rate, blood oxygen saturation, and body temperature. We examine commissioning, attribute retrieval, communication under simulated packet loss, and operational traffic through a reference controller. The study demonstrates that this custom telemetry model can operate within the tested Matter configuration without modifying commissioning or secure-session mechanisms. The resulting testbed gives researchers and device developers a concrete platform for investigating health-oriented extensions. It also supports exploration of local telemetry integration that could help patients and caregivers access readings across connected devices.

</details>


### [274] [Vector-Space Projection and Unified Complex-Valued Acceleration for Scaling Matrix-Based Brain-Inspired Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16363)
*William Youngwoo Chung,Hyunwoo Oh,Calvin Yeung,Hansen Jin Lillemark,Hamza Errahmouni Barkam,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于GHRR的扁平化方法，使其无需通过置换逻辑即可进行训练和推理，同时设计了一种专用于融合GHRR绑定和相似性的ASIC，实现了比GPU更高的吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着符号推理规模的应用需求增加，亟需找到一种既能表达复杂信息又能高效硬件实现的表示方法。现有的FHRR不适合表示顺序、图形和层次结构的原因是在绑定时引入了成本高昂的置换逻辑，而GHRR虽然能够自然编码这些结构，但因此增加了计算和存储开销，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 本文提出了一种扁平化方法，它将GHRR基于矩阵的表示转换为向量空间执行，实现了与FHRR推理功能等效但避免了置换逻辑。同时，设计了一种专用于融合GHRR绑定和相似性的28nm ASIC，包含双DMA流和运行时归一化机制。

Result: 相较于基于PyTorch的NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU基准，该集成电路原型实现了1.36-1.56倍的更高吞吐量和16.2-18.6倍的更好能效。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过扁平化方法和专有ASIC的结合，可以高效实现GHRR表示，为高维度计算应用提供了一种有前景的硬件实现方案。

Abstract: Symbolic reasoning at scale requires representations that are both expressive and hardware-efficient. Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) provides a promising brain-inspired framework, encoding concepts as high-dimensional vectors composed through efficient algebraic operations. However, the widely used Fourier Holographic Reduced Representation (FHRR) relies on commutative binding, which cannot encode order or directionality. Prior FPGA/ASIC accelerators recover this through costly permutation logic, incurring irregular memory access and high control overhead. Generalized Holographic Reduced Representations (GHRR) instead redefine binding as non-commutative matrix multiplication, naturally encoding sequences, graphs, and hierarchies without permutations-but at the cost of turning every element into a matrix, introducing compute and storage overhead that limits scalability. In this work, we propose a flattening methodology that preserves GHRR's matrix-based encoding while executing training and inference directly in vector space, functionally equivalent to FHRR inference yet free of permutation logic. We further design a custom 28 nm ASIC that fuses binding and similarity into a unified complex-valued datapath, with dual-DMA streaming and runtime normalization for accurate inference. Against a PyTorch baseline on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU, the prototype delivers 1.36x-1.56x higher throughput and 16.2x-18.6x better energy efficiency.

</details>


### [275] [Infrastructure for Verifiable AI: Where Proof Costs Go and How Hardware Can Help](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17042)
*Yihan Xia,Wenxin Xu,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四层框架，用于验证AI工作负载、证明系统、加密内核和硬件平台之间的联系。通过对现有文献的回顾和实测六种证明者和七种内核的成本，发现不同证明系统和输入长度下主要成本的变化规律。研究揭示了显存容量与执行时间的关联，并指出了现有算子模型的不足，通过实测确定了推进研究议程的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确保人工智能服务（使用大规模语言模型和其他变压器模型）在推断和训练过程中遵循声明的计算流程，且不泄露私有模型或数据，研究零知识证明在AI领域中的应用。现有硬件加速研究对单个加密操作的收益进行了报告，但对神经网络作业的整体成本影响不清晰，因此需要一个综合框架来分析。

Method: 本文提出了一种四层框架，结合文献回顾和最新的硬件平台实测数据，评估不同证明系统和输入长度下的主要成本。

Result: 研究表明，证明开销主要随证明系统和输入长度变化。低秩微调任务中，固定权重承诺的成本几乎不变，而中间值相关的开销随着输入长度增加。13亿参数的微调配置超出了40GB显存设备的能力，但在80GB设备上可以完成。研究还确定了流水线和同步限制，并提出了一系列优化措施。

Conclusion: 研究结果指明了硬件和证明系统优化的选择应依据目标工作负载的成本特征，未来研究应结合实测瓶颈推进加密格式、固定权重承诺复用、异构执行和共享基准测试的研究议程。

Abstract: Users of artificial intelligence services, including those powered by large language models and other transformer-based models, need assurance that inference and training follow the declared computation without exposing private models or data. Zero-knowledge proofs can provide this assurance, but proof generation remains expensive. Hardware acceleration research often reports gains for individual cryptographic operations, leaving their effect on the full cost of proving neural-network workloads, particularly large transformer models, unclear. We introduce a four-layer framework linking verifiable AI workloads, proof systems, cryptographic kernels and hardware platforms, use it to review the literature, and add original measurements of six provers and seven kernels to identify which costs matter in practice. Our measurements show that the dominant cost changes with the proof system and input length. In low-rank fine-tuning, the cost of opening commitments to fixed model weights remains nearly constant across input lengths from 64 to 2048, while the work associated with intermediate values grows. A 13-billion-parameter fine-tuning configuration exceeds the capacity of a 40-gigabyte graphics processor but completes on an 80-gigabyte device, showing why capacity must be assessed alongside execution time. Our kernel profiling further identifies instruction issue and synchronization limits that a bandwidth-only model misses. We use these findings to assess graphics processors, reconfigurable logic, custom chips and existing artificial intelligence accelerators. The resulting research agenda connects proof-friendly numeric formats, reuse of fixed-weight commitments, heterogeneous execution and shared benchmarks to the measured bottlenecks. Effective acceleration requires selecting hardware and proof-system optimizations around the cost profile of the target workload.

</details>


### [276] [Broadcast, Don't Replicate: Eliminating Weight HBM in Datacenter LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17097)
*Hannah Atmer,Yuan Yao,Thiemo Voigt,Stefanos Kaxiras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Optical Broadcast Memory (OBM)的技术，它通过一种具有可扩展性的光纤架构来广播模型参数，从而解决了大规模数据中心中大规模语言模型推理的内存容量和带宽限制问题。与基于光环的方案相比，OBM利用动态缓冲大小来适应不同的上下文长度，并展示了其在数据中心规模下相比基于HBM的系统在吞吐量和每标记能耗方面的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据中心规模下大规模语言模型推理中，传统的内存复制架构由于读取只写参数且需在成千上万的加速器本地DRAM中复制参数，导致高昂的成本、大量的静态功耗和冗余的内存获取能量。为此，研究提出了Optical Broadcast Memory（OBM）技术来解决这个问题。

Method: OBM采用了一种中心化内存服务器广播参数的方法，即通过将只读光学元件与计算单元共封装，使数据流在分布式推理节点以被动方式摄取，从而直接将参数传送到计算单元，无需中间缓冲。此外，OBM还采用了可变大小的缓冲区来弥补连续流参数传输的局限性。

Result: 在80,000个加速器的数据中心规模下，OBM通过删除光环的约束条件，成功地服务了具备专家混合（MoE）模型。实验结果表明，OBM能够消除使用基于HBM存储的系统中所需的PB级HBM，同时在吞吐量和每标记能耗方面优于HBM系统。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用OBM技术在大规模语言模型推理方面的能耗和效果提升明显，能够有效降低相关的功耗和开支。这一技术有望在未来大规模数据处理和AI应用中得到应用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference at datacenter scale is fundamentally bottlenecked by memory capacity and bandwidth limits. Because model weights are read-only and identical across instances, conventional architectures that replicate these parameters across the local DRAM of tens of thousands of accelerators incur massive capital expenditures, significant static power dissipation, and redundant memory-fetch energy. A recent proposal to use optical fiber loops as delay-line memory alleviates these issues, but its recirculating loop must hold the whole model: loop length grows with model size, slack is fixed when the ring is built, and the layouts that serve modern Mixture of Experts (MoE) models efficiently do not fit in it.
  We propose Optical Broadcast Memory (OBM), an open-ended hierarchical Tap-and-Amplify fiber architecture that generalizes and replaces the optical ring paradigm. A centralized memory server continuously broadcasts weights to distributed inference nodes that passively tap the data stream using receive-only co-packaged optics, delivering parameters directly to compute units without intermediate buffering. To overcome the limitations of continuous weight streaming, we use dynamically-sized slack to accommodate large and variable context lengths. We show how removing the restrictions of the optical ring allows OBM to efficiently serve MoE models. Evaluated at a datacenter scale of 80,000 accelerators, OBM eliminates petabytes of HBM while being competitive or better in throughput and energy per token than an HBM-based system.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Zugwise: Domain-Aware Game Reconstruction for Digitizing Handwritten Chess Scoresheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14544)
*Gerhard Trippen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Zugwise的新方法，用于解码手写象棋评分表，通过复原棋局来处理错误，而不是仅检查单一的合法移动。与传统方法相比，此方法显著减少了沉默错误并提高了正确的修正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的手写象棋评分表识别方法过于关注移动的合法性，而遗漏了更为关键的错误，即那些生成了合法但错误移动的失误。这些失误会被后续的识别过程接受，并导致之后的移动建立在错误的基础上。因此，提出新的方法来解决这个问题。

Method: 研究将任务视为棋局复原，并引入一种称为Zugwise的方法，运用计算机象棋的工具（如静默搜索、静态交换评估、钉检测）来评估手写的移动是否合理可能。该方法包括错误检测器、反向搜索、候选排序以及合并对手方的示例。通过这些手段，它能更准确地识别并纠正手写数据中的错误。

Result: 通过在两个比赛部分的试用，Zugwise的表现显著优于传统方法。在新的实现版本中，沉默错误从16减少到1，从50减少到13。两段学习信号将正确的修复候选出现在替代决策中的比率从58.8%提高到78.0%。在第一个部分，经过人工验证的输出与独立专家的转录结果在45场比赛中的34场中完全一致，仲裁对评分表也发现了双方记录的不同错误。

Conclusion: 虽然此研究并未得出哪种工作流程更快或更准确的结论，但通过引入更为系统性的方法来处理手写象棋评分表，显著地提升了解码的准确性和可靠性，并提供了一种全新的处理方式。该系统完全在浏览器中运行，免费、私密且支持离线使用。同时，系统还提供了一种处理多记录不一致问题的协议。

Abstract: Digitizing handwritten chess scoresheets has been studied as a handwriting-recognition problem: read the move in each box and check that it is legal. This framing misses the errors that matter most. A misread that yields an illegal move is loud. One that yields a legal-but-wrong move is silent, because a legality-only pipeline accepts it and reads every later move against a wrong position. We therefore treat the task as game reconstruction and present Zugwise, which repurposes computer-chess machinery (quiescence search, static exchange evaluation, pin detection) to ask not "what should be played?" but "could a human plausibly have written this?" An absurdity detector makes silent errors loud at or near where they occur, and a backward search traces late symptoms to their cause. Candidate ranking excludes chess-quality signals. A Needleman-Wunsch merge adds the second player's sheet as evidence, and review stops send every disagreement between the sheets' legal readings to a human. In oracle replay of two tournament sections, a release adding them cuts silent errors from 16 to 1 and from 50 to 13. Two learned signals, CTC re-scoring against the recognizer's raw output and a Maia-style human-move prior, raise the correct fix's top-rank rate from 58.8% to 78.0% of the replacement decisions where it is a candidate, and hold without refitting on a later section. On the first section, the human-verified output matches an independent expert transcription move for move on 34 of 45 games, and adjudication against the sheets found errors in both records, on different plies. We do not establish that either workflow is faster or more accurate. The system runs entirely in the browser and is free, private, and offline-capable; alongside it we offer a three-way adjudication protocol for a domain where no single record is ground truth.

</details>


### [278] [Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14654)
*Egor Pakhomov,Anurag Koul,Hongjun Liu,Bo Pang,Shafiq Joty,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 此研究通过修改历史记录或世界状态，分析这些修改如何影响接收模型的表现。研究发现，错误的记录方式可以提高或降低接收模型的成功率，具体取决于世界的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨工具使用语言模型代理的历史记录方式（包括记录错误纠正步骤或直接记录完成的动作）与其后来接收模型表现之间的关系。

Method: 研究使用了AgentCE-Bench 和 AppWorld 两个基准平台，在特定任务中冻结代理的进度，并对历史记录或世界状态进行编辑，观察这些编辑如何影响接收模型的表现。

Result: 在AgentCE-Bench中，添加正确的值通过两种自然编辑（附近的正确笔记或代理自身已完成的操作）提高了接收模型的成功率，从51%提高到66%。而在AppWorld中，不同的收据报告（成功或失败）以及目标效果是否出现在世界中，对任务整体成功率的影响显著不同，成功收据降低了成功率，而目标效果的出现则提高了成功率。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，历史记录编辑的效果依赖于描述的世界状态。在世界状态仍然存在错误的情况下，为了使历史记录看起来干净而忽略真实情况，可能会使代理器的表现变得更差。这表明在传达代理行为的信息时，既要确保历史记录的准确性和完整性，也要考虑到接收模型理解的真实环境状态。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.

</details>


### [279] [Together Compiler: Continual Compilation for AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14681)
*Reyna Abhyankar,Dylan Lim,Drew Wadsworth,Venkata Hasith Vattikuti,YuTong Julia Zhang,Jung Hwan Heo,Taeksang Kim,Kesavan Ramakrishnan,Evangelene Stanley,Xinyu Fang,Daniel Y. Fu,Simran Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Together Compiler (TKCC) 是一个专为 AI 推断优化设计的持续代理循环系统。通过 Tiered Persistent Engine 和成本模型，TKCC 有效解决了吞吐量和精度之间的权衡问题，并在多种硬件平台上超越了其他优化算法，特别是在吞吐量方面表现卓越，适用于广泛的工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工作负载变化迅速：新模型频繁发布，模型架构趋于多样化，流量分布也常变。传统的优化方法无法满足这些变化的性能要求，特别是在吞吐量和精度之间取得平衡。现有的系统往往在这两者之间做出妥协，或者优化效率低。因此，开发一个适合持续优化、能够兼顾高吞吐量和高精度的方法具有重要意义。

Method: TKCC 通过以下创新方法解决了传统优化方法的局限性：1) Tiered Persistent Engine 分离了引擎启动开销和前向计算，从而提高了优化效率；2) 成本模型用于选择优化过程中的并行性配置，以提高整体性能。

Result: 在 Llama 1B 和后续的语言模型上的测试表明，TKCC 在各种硬件平台上吞吐量较手写优化的 MegaKernels 高出 1.7 倍，较最新开源推断引擎高出 2.6 倍。此外，TKCC 在单批次大小下的性能达到了最新的行业标准，分别在 M3.1 Flash 和 DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 上达到了 1111 次/秒和 1205 次/秒。

Conclusion: Together Compiler (TKCC) 通过对推断优化流程的创新处理，不仅提升了性能，也为大规模部署提供了灵活性，证明了其在AI模型优化方面的领先地位。

Abstract: Today's AI workloads are rapidly changing: new models are released more often, model architectures are increasingly diverse, and traffic distributions change frequently. The challenge we face at Together AI is continual optimization for these changing workloads. Compiling a deployment into an optimized forward pass is a challenge that is well-suited for agent hill-climbing. Existing systems struggle with continual compilation due to a tradeoff between measurement throughput and fidelity. Many systems use agents to optimize individual kernels from the inference forward pass using offline benchmarks, but timings are low-fidelity because of data-dependent behavior in primitives like mixture-of-experts (MoE). On the other hand, systems use agents to directly optimize end-to-end engines but suffer from slow engine launch times. To address these challenges, we build Together Compiler (TKCC), the first truly continual agent loop designed for AI inference optimization. To address the throughput-fidelity tradeoff, we propose: (1) a Tiered Persistent Engine that decouples engine launch overheads from forward pass computation; (2) a cost model to select the parallelism configurations that seed the optimization process. We also conduct extensive ablations on agent context management and present our insights on deploying TKCC in production. TKCC outperforms highly-optimized hand-written Megakernels on the Llama 1B workhorse workload by up to 1.7x and the best open source engines on the latest frontier models (Kimi K3, GLM 5.3, MiniMax M3.1 Flash, DeepSeek v4.1 Flash) across hardware platforms by up to 2.6x higher TPS on the InferenceX benchmark. TKCC also sets the state-of-the-art performance at batch size 1, with 1111 TPS on M3.1 Flash and 1205 TPS on DeepSeek v4.1 Flash. Finally, TKCC generalizes beyond frontier language model inference. TKCC serves trillions of tokens across our production fleet.

</details>


### [280] [When Should Semantic Priors Enter Partially-Identified Causal Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14736)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Saad Bin Ather,Valentin Six,Lancelot Da Costa,Tommaso Salvatori,Bernhard Scholkopf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个框架来区分因果关系的总体非识别性和样本不稳定性，使用语义信息评估因果方向，但发现语义信息在分配和保留方面不可靠


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决因果方向的不确定性问题，提高因果图恢复的准确性

Method: 利用大型语言模型（LLMs）的语义信息，评估语义方向，并通过基准网络实验验证效果

Result: 语义信息在因果图恢复中优于随机方向，但针对不稳定边的集中查询没有显著优势；现有的接纳证书无法有效接纳复杂的因果图；下游保留依赖于参考的准确性，学习到的参考只能正确包含真实的DAG的三分之一，语义信息的价值与其分配、接纳和保留的可靠性分离

Conclusion: 研究凸显了语义信息在因果发现中的价值，但也揭示了分配、接纳和保留中的可靠性问题

Abstract: Observational causal discovery often leaves causal di- rections unresolved or unstable. Large language mod- els (LLMs) can provide semantic information to resolve these directions, but incorrect proposals can exclude the true causal graph and invalidate downstream inference. We study three separate decisions: where to allocate semantic queries, which proposals to admit, and which causal graphs to retain.
